Use instead: a supported split squat with lighter load or bodyweight at a challenging controlled depth. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between leg bouts; 10 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 1 · P3](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_01.md). |
| P2 | **Push-Up — controlled full repetition** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Strengthen the press with shoulder blades free to move and the trunk held as one unit, complementing the floor-supported press. Use 2 seconds down, a decisive ascent and 1 second at the top; finish by pushing the floor away without adding a separate scapular cycle. Choose hand elevation or band resistance so 8 reps leave 2–3 in reserve. No hand flight. Use instead: higher stable hand support or appropriate resistance to retain the same controlled reps and technical reserve. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 1 · P3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_01.md). |
| P3 | **Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Plantar-flexion force while the knee remains flexed during early push and receiving. Sit on an inspected stable bench, feet on floor; secure loads on thighs, rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s without knee-angle change or bouncing. Use instead: a supported standing bent-knee calf raise with the same bilateral dose and 2–3 technical RIR if bench/load support is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 1 · P5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_01.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Hammer Curl — simultaneous arms** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Give elbow flexors direct loading for completing the draw in rows without another shoulder-extension set. Stand still, neutral grips, upper arms at sides; lift in 1 second, hold 1 second and lower for 2 seconds. No hip swing or wrist collapse. Both arms move together; this is 8 bilateral cycles, not 8 alternating reps. Use instead: lighter dumbbells with the same simultaneous neutral-grip curl and controlled range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 1 · P6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_01.md). |
| P5 | **Loaded Cossack Squat** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a substantial lateral knee-and-hip compound, strengthening weight acceptance and return over the stance leg that supports the selected lower rotational action. Hold a suitable dumbbell at the chest in a comfortable wide stance. Sit toward the working hip as the other leg lengthens, keeping the loaded foot supported and the knee following the toes. Return to centre under control. Use about 2 seconds lowering, 1 second lifting and 1 second organising the next rep. Depth is individually qualified; do not force a deep groin stretch or turn on a fixed knee. Use instead: a shallower controlled Cossack range and adjust the dumbbell load for knee or pelvic-position faults. Stop for groin pain or inability to qualify a challenging controlled range; do not add a second movement. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 1 · P3](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_01.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Cable / Band Chop — standing split-stance band execution** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the downward diagonal arm path and its controlled return, supporting rotational hand acceleration and braking without adding another release. Secure a band at a verified high anchor to one side. Stand in a comfortable split stance with the opposite foot forward. With both hands, move from above the anchor-side shoulder toward the opposite front pocket while feet and hips turn through a modest arc. Drive in 1–2 seconds and return in 3 seconds. Keep the trunk long; avoid sideways crunching and band recoil. No cable machine is assumed. Use instead: Reduce band resistance or diagonal range if the athlete side-bends or loses control. If a suitable secure high anchor is unavailable, stop and re-audit the slot; do not improvise an unsafe anchor. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 1 · P4](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_01.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

**Preparation sets, separate from working:**

- **P1 — Split Squat — stationary, two dumbbells:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per leg; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds, 30 s between legs; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P2 — Push-Up — controlled full repetition:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 3. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P3 — Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P4 — Dumbbell Hammer Curl — simultaneous arms:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P5 — Loaded Cossack Squat:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
- **P6 — Cable / Band Chop — standing split-stance band execution:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

**Workload:** 7 nonrotational and 4 rotational ball releases, including 0 nonrotational downward slams and 4 rotational slams already included in those release totals; 0 source ball-receiving events and 0 rotational handoffs counted separately. 3 bilateral / 0 unilateral foot-landings (6 individual landing-foot contacts), with 0 low-amplitude events already included; 0 unilateral takeoffs. 0 bilateral hand-receptions / 0 hand contacts. 4 grounded rapid leg reps, 4 grounded lower-rotation efforts (including 0 kick actions) and 0 retained/unloaded upper-rotation efforts. 0 short starts / 0 accelerating metres; 0 counted lower source-force walking-entry steps and 0 counted upper source-force task steps. Rotational entry/recovery foot placements belong to complete rotational attempts and are not separately enumerated. Primary: 6 rounds / 9 side-specific bouts, 54 conventional/one-way-traverse reps plus 0 complete walkout cycles (0 heel placements). Preparation: 30 reps/traverses plus 0 walkout cycles, separate from work. Light supports: 16 reps/cycles. These mixed units are prescribed descriptions, not equivalent stress or actual completed work.

**Delivery and timing:** Estimate 60–67 minutes after Access & Prepare 1 with prompt equipment access. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Lateral step-in to rotational plant changes the rotational start from a fixed stance to one controlled lateral arrival, and Med Ball Overhead to Side Slam — non-rebounding slam-ball execution gives that organized base a diagonal hand-force endpoint. The broad jump and shallow-countermovement chest pass retain forward projection as support; the rotational plant is a grounded step, not an added landing. Compared with Class 2, prescribed ball releases change from 7 to 11, bilateral foot-landing events change from 0 to 3, bilateral hand-receiving events change from 3 to 0. Progression remains conditional on actual recovery, feet that realign with the pelvis and a balanced stop before the next turn. Shorten only the remaining plant attempts if the step overreaches; use the listed shoulder-height diagonal slam for remaining E2 attempts if overhead range requires rib flare. Preserve the side recovery and contained impact setup.

Previous combined Class 2 prescribed 12 primary bouts and 64 conventional/traverse reps plus 3 cycles; this class prescribes 9 bouts, 54 reps and 0 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. This third lesson completes stage coverage and repeats the selected light supports for the stated shoulder/hip/foot jobs. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. The extra light supports are disclosed repeats rather than new work identities. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_03_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_03.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

**Handoff:** Review actual delivery and this saved workload before Class 4; develop its complementary job without assuming these prescriptions were performed.

## Full Body Force Generation — Class 4 of 36

**Stage 2, lesson A: Hip-driven release and arm-only projection**

**Primary effort:** Project from the hips, then isolate the forward arm release from unnecessary leg assistance.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — paused hinge start as the controlled lower-body force anchor. Project from the hips, then isolate the forward arm release from unnecessary leg assistance.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — paused hinge start. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Hip force with a controlled trunk:** Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — paused hinge start and Kneeling Medicine Ball Chest Pass — tall kneeling, no hip swing: Establish a steady trunk before projection; allow the scoop's hip drive while preserving the kneeling pass's arm-led release.
- **E3 + E4 — Supported traction and hip extension:** Glute Bridge — fast grounded rise, settled floor start and Seated Hand-over-Hand Battle-Rope Pull — free rope on floor: Use the available ground support to stabilize the body while the arms draw or the hips extend; avoid finishing by leaning or arching the back.
- **E5 + E6 — Hip initiation into hand force:** Lead-leg low roundhouse without a gather step and Light-band rotational cross: Forward rotational punch paired with lead-leg hip-led output.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — paused hinge start** | **2 × 3** | Main forward force release driven by hip extension from a held hinge. Hold a light ball between thighs in a comfortable hinge for 2 s; extend hips rapidly and release forward/upward with long arms. No extra dip, arm curl, jump or twist; no return catch. Use instead: a rapid light unloaded hip hinge at the same dose if containment fails, with zero throws recorded. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between complete set rounds; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E2 | **Kneeling Medicine Ball Chest Pass — tall kneeling, no hip swing** | **2 × 3** | Primary straight-ahead release with leg drive removed. Knees on a comfortable surface, hips extended; hold ball at chest for 1 s, release rapidly with both arms and remain tall. Use instead: a seated chest pass if kneeling is uncomfortable, or a self-anchored band speed chest press if no release area exists; log the changed task and any lost throws. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between complete set rounds; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E3 | **Glute Bridge — fast grounded rise, settled floor start** | **1 × 4** | Express hip extension near its finish without another lengthened hinge or landing. Bodyweight/light secured pelvic load; settle hips gently on floor, drive up quickly, hold 1 s, lower 2 s. Keep feet down and knees still; finish without back arch. Use instead: an unloaded smaller-range fast bridge if load handling or pelvic control is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E3](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E4 | **Seated Hand-over-Hand Battle-Rope Pull — free rope on floor** | **1 × 8** | Link a secure grip to rapid horizontal traction, transferring the draw between arms. Lay a manageable free rope length straight within the inspected lane; sit, brace feet, alternate 4 short pulls per arm without leaning backward. Regrip after each pull; rope never wraps around the body. No fixed anchor or attached weight. Use instead: an alternating seated band row with permitted foot anchoring if rope mass/friction or lane clearance prevents controlled quick pulls; record the lost rope-transfer task. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 2 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E5 | **Lead-leg low roundhouse without a gather step** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Directly train front-leg release while the rear leg supplies the rotational base. Start with the kicking leg in front. Shift onto the rear support leg, allow its foot to pivot, then execute one low roundhouse with a soft terminal knee, active recoil and a return to the starting stance. Do not shuffle the rear foot forward first. A qualified coach must establish the low noncontact execution. Keep the foot below hip height, support foot turning with the pelvis, knee softly extended and recoil active; no target impact, jump, spin or locked-knee snap. Use instead: a lower chamber/shorter extension instead if the athlete needs an extra gather or leans away; do not speed up a trapped rear-foot pivot. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 2 · E2](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E6 | **Light-band rotational cross** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Transmit the turn into a fast forward punch. From a modest staggered stance, anchor behind the rear shoulder; turn the rear foot/hip then drive the hand forward at shoulder height. Stop short of locked elbow and return under control; never release the band. Verify the stated band anchor and tension; retain the band throughout, with no release or uncontrolled recoil. Use instead: Reduce tension or reach if the wrist folds or arm leads the turn. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 2 · E1](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Supine Dumbbell Serratus Reach — elbows straight** | **1 × 8** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Lightly load scapular reach at the press finish while the floor supports trunk position. Very light paired dumbbells above chest; keep elbows straight, reach shoulder blades slightly off floor 2 s, hold 1 s, return 2 s without rolling the trunk. Use instead: an unloaded supine reach through the same comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · S1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| S2 | **Side-Lying Hip Abduction — light** | **1 × 5 per leg** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light lateral-hip strengthening to keep the pelvis and knee organized over the force-producing leg. Lie on side, lower leg bent; lift upper leg slightly 2 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s with toes facing forward and pelvis still. Bodyweight is sufficient unless clearly easy. Use instead: a smaller unloaded leg lift with the same per-leg dose. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 10 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · S1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Romanian Deadlift — two dumbbells** | **2 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Hip extension from a lengthened hinge, with the trunk transmitting rather than supplying the motion. Lower 3 s with soft knees, bells close and hips back; stand in about 1 s. Stop range before back rounding or pelvic tuck. Use instead: a lighter two-dumbbell hinge through a shorter controlled range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s between complete set rounds; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P2 | **Floor Press — two dumbbells, neutral grip** | **2 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Main horizontal pressing force with a reproducible floor-limited range. Lower 2 s, touch upper arms gently, pause 1 s, then press decisively; feet stay down. Coach supervises pickup/set-down. Use instead: a lighter floor press through controlled comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 150 s between complete set rounds; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P3 | **Hamstring Walkout — complete out-and-back cycle** | **1 × 3 cycles** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Knee-flexor leverage while the pelvis stays raised, distinct from moving through a hip hinge. Start in a bilateral bridge. Each cycle is two small heel steps out per foot, then two back per foot: eight placements in about 8 s. Keep a soft knee and level hips; never chase a fully straight leg. Use instead: a shorter-lever walkout at the same complete-cycle count; reduce range for sagging or cramp and stop if control does not return. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P4 | **Band Lat Pulldown — kneeling, two arms** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the downward arm draw needed for assisted pull-up force without another horizontal row set. Use a manufacturer-permitted band on an inspected overhead anchor; kneel tall, draw elbows to ribs, hold 1 s, return 2 s; no lumbar lean. Use instead: a foot-supported pull-up with comfortable grip and enough assistance to preserve 2–3 RIR; otherwise a supported row with the vertical-pull gap recorded. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P4](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P5 | **Seated band hip internal rotation** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load controlled internal-rotation torque directly at the hip, complementing the larger leg push used to organise the selected grounded lower turn. Sit on a stable bench with thighs supported and hips and knees near 90 degrees. Fit a secure loop around the lower shins. Keep the other foot planted while the working foot moves a short distance outward, lightly clear of the floor, through hip internal rotation. Keep that knee and the pelvis still. Take 2 seconds outward and 2 seconds returning; do not force end range or twist the knee or ankle. Use instead: Reduce range or band tension for knee, ankle or pelvic movement. If the loop cannot be fitted securely, use self-resisted seated hip internal rotation with the opposite hand resisting the outside of the lower shin instead; manual resistance is a changed and less reproducible load. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 2 · P4](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotational Press** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a compound unilateral push for the full-body force work while loading the connection from a freely turning foot and hip to a controlled arm press. Its diagonal pressing path complements the selected rotational task; it does not reproduce every throw path. Secure the landmine and qualify its bar, grip and minimum load. In a modest staggered stance, hold the sleeve near the rear shoulder with the rear hand. Turn the rear foot and hips into a controlled forward-and-up press, keeping wrist stacked and ribs level. No dip-drive, jump or release. Return in 3 seconds and reset without shoulder shrug or lower-back arch. Use instead: Reduce load or range for wrist, elbow or rib-position faults. If the minimum landmine setup is unsuitable, use a one-arm rotational band press from a verified anchor instead; log the changed load and setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 2 · P1](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

- **P5 — Seated band hip internal rotation:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

**Workload:** 12 nonrotational and 0 rotational ball releases, including 0 nonrotational downward slams and 0 rotational slams already included in those release totals; 0 source ball-receiving events and 0 rotational handoffs counted separately. 0 bilateral / 0 unilateral foot-landings (0 individual landing-foot contacts), with 0 low-amplitude events already included; 0 unilateral takeoffs. 0 bilateral hand-receptions / 0 hand contacts. 4 grounded rapid leg reps, 4 grounded lower-rotation efforts (including 4 kick actions) and 4 retained/unloaded upper-rotation efforts. 0 short starts / 0 accelerating metres; 0 counted lower source-force walking-entry steps and 0 counted upper source-force task steps. Rotational entry/recovery foot placements belong to complete rotational attempts and are not separately enumerated. Primary: 8 rounds / 10 side-specific bouts, 52 conventional/one-way-traverse reps plus 3 complete walkout cycles (24 heel placements). Preparation: 34 reps/traverses plus 1 walkout cycles, separate from work. Light supports: 18 reps/cycles. These mixed units are prescribed descriptions, not equivalent stress or actual completed work.

**Delivery and timing:** Estimate 68–75 minutes after Access & Prepare 1 with prompt equipment access. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — paused hinge start holds the hinge before release, making hip-force initiation explicit; Kneeling Medicine Ball Chest Pass — tall kneeling, no hip swing removes hip swing to preserve a distinct arm-led forward release. The grounded bridge and eight total hand-over-hand rope draws support hip completion and traction without another receiving task. Compared with Class 3, prescribed ball releases change from 11 to 12, bilateral foot-landing events change from 3 to 0. After checking actual recovery and release-area suitability, require the paused hinge to remain organized and the kneeling pass to finish tall. If containment fails, use the listed rapid unloaded hip hinge for remaining scoop attempts and log zero releases; a seated chest pass is the listed remaining-dose alternative when kneeling contact is unsuitable. Do not compress retrieval or side rest.

Previous combined Class 3 prescribed 9 primary bouts and 54 conventional/traverse reps plus 0 cycles; this class prescribes 10 bouts, 52 reps and 3 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_04_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_04.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

**Handoff:** Review actual delivery and this saved workload before Class 5; develop its complementary job without assuming these prescriptions were performed.

## Full Body Force Generation — Class 5 of 36

**Stage 2, lesson B: Guided push and supported ascent**

**Primary effort:** Accelerate a guided arm push and a supported upward effort.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Landmine Press — one-arm strict speed press as the controlled upper-body force anchor. Accelerate a guided arm push and a supported upward effort.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Landmine Press — one-arm strict speed press. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Drive from an organized base:** Landmine Press — one-arm strict speed press and Kettlebell Swing — continuous two-hand chest-height float: Keep the support pressure organized as force begins; the strict press uses no leg dip, while the retained swing expresses its deliberate hip impulse.
- **E3 + E4 — Supported upward force:** Foot-Supported Pull-Up — fast ascent, controlled return and Seated Vertical Jump — low bench start to floor landing: Drive upward from the declared support without rocking for momentum, then control the return or two-foot reception.
- **E5 + E6 — Supported rotational force:** Pre-chambered bent-knee roundhouse whip and Two-hand band backhand speed sweep: Transverse hand sweep and short-lever thigh sweep express rotation without more release or terminal kick volume.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Landmine Press — one-arm strict speed press** | **1 × 3 per arm** | Accelerate a unilateral push against a guided load while the body resists turning. Standing feet fixed, bar end at shoulder; press forward/up rapidly without a knee dip, then lower 2 s; maintain grip throughout. Use instead: a light self-anchored single-arm band press with the other hand securing the tail at the torso if the bar is too heavy; no band release. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between arm bouts; 6 total reps; 5 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E2 | **Kettlebell Swing — continuous two-hand chest-height float** | **1 × 4** | Reapply hip force after the retained implement returns, with no ground landing. Use a light bell and established hinge; four continuous hip-driven swings to chest-level float. Guide the return, keep ribs stacked and park after the fourth. No arm lift or squat bounce. Use instead: four separated parked swings with full 10 s resets if continuous timing is unconfirmed; add actual time. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E3 | **Foot-Supported Pull-Up — fast ascent, controlled return** | **1 × 3** | Express a rapid bodyweight pull from above while leg support keeps arm speed controllable. Use an inspected pull-up bar and immovable box so feet remain supported throughout; overhand shoulder-width grip, no kip. Pull up quickly, lower 2 s, reset without jumping or dropping. Use instead: a rapid Band Lat Pulldown on an inspected rated overhead attachment if the bar/support geometry is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 5 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E4](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E4 | **Seated Vertical Jump — low bench start to floor landing** | **1 × 3** | Rebuild upward force from a supported start without a preceding downward dip. Sit lightly on an inspected low bench with feet fully planted in front. Pause 2 s, incline torso only enough to rise, then jump upward and land on the clear floor. No backward rock or collapse onto the seat; walk to reset. Use instead: a settled quarter-squat jump if bench fit or getting clear of it is unsuitable; record the repeated Week 1 task. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E4](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E5 | **Pre-chambered bent-knee roundhouse whip** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Isolate proximal thigh/pelvis acceleration without the initial lifting or knee-extension task. Begin with the free thigh already lifted below hip height and knee folded. Briskly sweep the thigh through a small roundhouse arc as the support foot turns; keep the knee folded throughout, then return the foot to stance. Let the support foot or both feet turn freely with the pelvis; never force rotation through a trapped knee. Remain grounded. Use instead: Lower the starting chamber or reduce arc for trunk lean; the standing knee must turn with the foot, not remain trapped. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 2 · E5](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E6 | **Two-hand band backhand speed sweep** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Accelerate the transverse pulling/sweeping path while controlling its endpoint. Anchor at chest height on the starting side. Both hands begin near that side of the chest; pivot and sweep across toward the opposite side with softly bent elbows, stopping within a comfortable arc. Return slowly. Verify the stated band anchor and tension; retain the band throughout, with no release or uncontrolled recoil. Use instead: Reduce tension/arc for side bending or a jerky return. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 2 · E3](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Bilateral Band External Rotation — elbows at sides** | **1 × 8** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light shoulder-rotator strengthening to control upper-arm position during the day's press and pull. Unanchored band between hands, elbows at ribs; rotate outward 2 s, hold 1 s, return 2 s without shrugging. Use instead: a lower-tension band and shorter comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · S2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| S2 | **Seated Band Ankle Eversion — light** | **1 × 5 per leg** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light outer-ankle strengthening to resist rolling over the foot edge during the push. Heel supported; hand-held light band draws the forefoot inward. Move forefoot outward 2 s, hold 1 s, return 2 s while the knee stays still. Use instead: gentle hand resistance through a smaller range at the same per-leg dose. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 10 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · S2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Goblet Squat — dumbbell** | **1 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Bilateral knee/hip extension through a repeatable full-foot push. Lower 3 s, stand decisively in about 1 s; hold a manageable goblet load and retain comfortable depth without heels lifting. Use instead: a lighter goblet squat through controlled comfortable range; a two-dumbbell front hold may replace a limiting goblet grip. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P2 | **One-Arm Row — dumbbell, hand and knee on bench** | **2 × 6 per arm** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the arm-driven backward draw while support prevents torso rotation. Draw elbow toward hip, hold 1 s, lower 2 s; stop before the shoulder rolls forward or the trunk turns. Use instead: a thigh-supported dumbbell row with the same arm path if a bench does not fit. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s between complete set rounds; 60 s between arm bouts; 24 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P3 | **Standing Dumbbell Calf Raise — straight knees** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Plantar-flexion force with the knees extended for the final upward/forward push. Use stable hand support if needed; rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s to the floor. Keep knees softly straight and heels tracking over the forefoot. Use instead: a lighter supported standing calf raise; use individual load or leverage that retains the required technical reserve. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P4](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Skull Crusher — floor-supported** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load elbow extension for the finish of a forceful press. Neutral grips, upper arms near vertical; lower 2 s beside the ears, extend under control without shoulder swing. Use instead: a lighter floor-supported elbow extension through comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P5](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P5 | **Seated band hip internal rotation** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load controlled internal-rotation torque directly at the hip, complementing the larger leg push used to organise the selected grounded lower turn. Sit on a stable bench with thighs supported and hips and knees near 90 degrees. Fit a secure loop around the lower shins. Keep the other foot planted while the working foot moves a short distance outward, lightly clear of the floor, through hip internal rotation. Keep that knee and the pelvis still. Take 2 seconds outward and 2 seconds returning; do not force end range or twist the knee or ankle. Use instead: Reduce range or band tension for knee, ankle or pelvic movement. If the loop cannot be fitted securely, use self-resisted seated hip internal rotation with the opposite hand resisting the outside of the lower shin instead; manual resistance is a changed and less reproducible load. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 2 · P4](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotation** | **1 × 6 alternating traverses total** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the turning arc and controlled endpoints that transmit lower-body force into the selected upper rotational action. Secure the landmine and qualify the bar, grip and minimum load. Hold the sleeve or suitable grip in both hands with elbows softly bent. Turn the feet and hips with a modest side-to-side loaded arc while keeping ribs over pelvis. Each one-way traverse is one rep; the return is the next counted rep. Take about 2 seconds per traverse and settle briefly at each endpoint. Avoid trunk lean, a trapped knee or an uncontrolled finish. Use instead: Reduce the load or arc for loss of alignment. If a secured, manageable landmine setup cannot be qualified, use a standing two-hand band rotation with a verified side anchor and the same one-way traverse counting instead; log the changed setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** Finish this working bout; no additional set or finisher is prescribed. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 2 · P4](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

**Preparation sets, separate from working:**

- **P1 — Goblet Squat — dumbbell:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 5; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 90 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P2 — One-Arm Row — dumbbell, hand and knee on bench:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5 per arm. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. - **P4 — Dumbbell Skull Crusher — floor-supported:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. - **P5 — Seated band hip internal rotation:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
- **P6 — Landmine Rotation:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4 alternating one-way traverses total, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
