## Full Body Force Generation — Class 6 of 36

**Stage 2, lesson C: Ankle rebound and overhead draw**

**Primary effort:** Organize a forward plant into a grounded hip turn and compact rotational hand arc. Ankle rebound, a supported split-leg rise and linear arm pressing/pulling support that base and finish.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Forward plant-and-open to kick stance as the grounded rotational force anchor. Accept forward arrival onto a leg before freeing the other thigh for a kick.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Forward plant-and-open to kick stance. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Turn and control the finish:** Forward plant-and-open to kick stance and Rotational shadow hook: Compact unloaded upper turn paired with forward arrival into an open lower stance.
- **E3 + E4 — Compact ankle and arm finish:** Ankle Pogo in Place — short bilateral set and Band Chest Press — simultaneous fast extension, no release: Keep the action compact and the support organized, with a clear ankle or arm finish; retain the pogo's brief contacts without speeding the band's return.
- **E5 + E6 — Stable-base upward force:** Split Squat — fast supported rise, no flight and Band Straight-Arm Pulldown — rapid shoulder extension: Hold the support stance steady while the leg rises or shoulders draw the arms down, with no trunk crunch or unwanted turn.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Forward plant-and-open to kick stance** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Accept forward arrival onto a leg before freeing the other thigh for a kick. Take one brisk short forward step onto the receiving leg and open the feet/pelvis into a shallow split kick stance, no more than 45 degrees from the initial facing. Keep the step and finish inside 2 m; no kick or run-up. Let the support foot or both feet turn freely with the pelvis; never force rotation through a trapped knee. Remain grounded. Use instead: Shorten the step if the athlete overstrides, stiffens the knee or drags a planted foot through the turn. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 2 · E6](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E2 | **Rotational shadow hook** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Coordinate a short transverse hand arc with the trunk turn. With no implement or target, keep elbow bent near 90 degrees and fist between chest and shoulder height; pivot and swing the forearm as a compact unit, stopping near the midline. Reset fully. Use instead: Shorten the arc if the fist outruns the trunk or shoulder rolls forward. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 2 · E2](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E3 | **Ankle Pogo in Place — short bilateral set** | **1 × 6** | Introduce a small elastic ankle contribution while retaining quiet low flights. Six low hops with softly extended knees and quick quiet receptions under the hips; no height chase. Absorb the sixth landing and stop, with no seventh bounce. Use instead: six grounded quick calf rises if rhythmic hand-free landing is unconfirmed; record zero landings. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E4 | **Band Chest Press — simultaneous fast extension, no release** | **1 × 4** | Continue pressing force into rising elastic tension through the finish. Band crosses upper back below neck, ends secured in hands using a permitted setup; feet fixed, fast forward press, controlled 2 s return without snapping elbows. Use instead: a non-flight fast incline push-up if no suitable self-anchored band is available. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 3 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E5](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E5 | **Split Squat — fast supported rise, no flight** | **1 × 2 per leg** | Apply one lead leg’s knee/hip force against a fixed stance without step timing. Light hand support, feet fixed in split stance; settle at a comfortable split-squat depth 2 s, rise quickly without leaving the floor and lower 2 s. Rear leg balances rather than launches. Use instead: a smaller supported rise at the same per-leg dose if stance control fades. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 4 total reps; 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| E6 | **Band Straight-Arm Pulldown — rapid shoulder extension** | **1 × 4** | Initiate the pull through shoulder extension without elbow flexion masking the action. Inspected overhead attachment; elbows softly fixed, pull hands from comfortable overhead reach to thighs quickly, return 2 s. Ribs stay quiet; no crunch or hip drive. Use instead: a light dumbbell pullover with controlled rapid return toward vertical and a shortened comfortable range if no rated anchor exists; log that it is not an equivalent speed test. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 3 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · E6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Bilateral Band External Rotation — elbows at sides** | **1 × 8** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light shoulder-rotator strengthening to control upper-arm position during the day's press and pull. Unanchored band between hands, elbows at ribs; rotate outward 2 s, hold 1 s, return 2 s without shrugging. Use instead: a lower-tension band and shorter comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · S2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| S2 | **Side-Lying Hip Abduction — light** | **1 × 5 per leg** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light lateral-hip strengthening to keep the pelvis and knee organized over the force-producing leg. Lie on side, lower leg bent; lift upper leg slightly 2 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s with toes facing forward and pelvis still. Bodyweight is sufficient unless clearly easy. Use instead: a smaller unloaded leg lift with the same per-leg dose. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 10 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · S1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Dumbbell Glute Bridge — floor-supported** | **1 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Hip extension near the finish with the knee angle held, without another lengthened-hinge dose. Secure a manageable load over the pelvis; rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s. Finish with ribs/pelvis aligned, not lumbar arching. Use instead: a lighter loaded floor bridge with the same controlled range; a shorter lever may be used if hamstrings cramp. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P3](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P2 | **Push-Up — controlled full repetition** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the press with free shoulder-blade movement and a steady trunk. Lower 2 s, push decisively, finish tall through shoulders for 1 s; no hand flight. Adjust leverage or band resistance to reach 2–3 RIR. Use instead: a hands-elevated push-up on an inspected immovable support. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P3 | **Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Plantar-flexion force while the knee remains flexed during early push and receiving. Sit on an inspected stable bench, feet on floor; secure loads on thighs, rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s without knee-angle change or bouncing. Use instead: a supported standing bent-knee calf raise with the same bilateral dose and 2–3 technical RIR if bench/load support is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Hammer Curl — simultaneous arms** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load elbow flexors for finishing the backward draw without another shoulder-extension set. Upper arms stay at ribs; lift 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s, without hip swing or wrist collapse. Use instead: a lighter neutral-grip curl without body movement. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 2 · P6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_02.md). |
| P5 | **Loaded Cossack Squat** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a substantial lateral knee-and-hip compound, strengthening weight acceptance and return over the stance leg that supports the selected lower rotational action. Hold a suitable dumbbell at the chest in a comfortable wide stance. Sit toward the working hip as the other leg lengthens, keeping the loaded foot supported and the knee following the toes. Return to centre under control. Use about 2 seconds lowering, 1 second lifting and 1 second organising the next rep. Depth is individually qualified; do not force a deep groin stretch or turn on a fixed knee. Use instead: a shallower controlled Cossack range and adjust the dumbbell load for knee or pelvic-position faults. Stop for groin pain or inability to qualify a challenging controlled range; do not add a second movement. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 2 · P3](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotation** | **1 × 6 alternating traverses total** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the turning arc and controlled endpoints that transmit lower-body force into the selected upper rotational action. Secure the landmine and qualify the bar, grip and minimum load. Hold the sleeve or suitable grip in both hands with elbows softly bent. Turn the feet and hips with a modest side-to-side loaded arc while keeping ribs over pelvis. Each one-way traverse is one rep; the return is the next counted rep. Take about 2 seconds per traverse and settle briefly at each endpoint. Avoid trunk lean, a trapped knee or an uncontrolled finish. Use instead: Reduce the load or arc for loss of alignment. If a secured, manageable landmine setup cannot be qualified, use a standing two-hand band rotation with a verified side anchor and the same one-way traverse counting instead; log the changed setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** Finish this working bout; no additional set or finisher is prescribed. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 2 · P4](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_02.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

**Preparation sets, separate from working:**

- **P1 — Dumbbell Glute Bridge — floor-supported:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P2 — Push-Up — controlled full repetition:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 3. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P3 — Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P4 — Dumbbell Hammer Curl — simultaneous arms:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P5 — Loaded Cossack Squat:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
- **P6 — Landmine Rotation:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4 alternating one-way traverses total, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

**Workload:** 0 nonrotational and 0 rotational ball releases, including 0 nonrotational downward slams and 0 rotational slams already included in those release totals; 0 source ball-receiving events and 0 rotational handoffs counted separately. 6 bilateral / 0 unilateral foot-landings (12 individual landing-foot contacts), with 6 low-amplitude events already included; 0 unilateral takeoffs. 0 bilateral hand-receptions / 0 hand contacts. 4 grounded rapid leg reps, 4 grounded lower-rotation efforts (including 0 kick actions) and 4 retained/unloaded upper-rotation efforts. 0 short starts / 0 accelerating metres; 0 counted lower source-force walking-entry steps and 0 counted upper source-force task steps. Rotational entry/recovery foot placements belong to complete rotational attempts and are not separately enumerated. Primary: 6 rounds / 7 side-specific bouts, 46 conventional/one-way-traverse reps plus 0 complete walkout cycles (0 heel placements). Preparation: 26 reps/traverses plus 0 walkout cycles, separate from work. Light supports: 18 reps/cycles. These mixed units are prescribed descriptions, not equivalent stress or actual completed work.

**Delivery and timing:** Estimate 53–60 minutes after Access & Prepare 1 with prompt equipment access. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Forward plant-and-open to kick stance makes controlled forward arrival into an open hip position the rotational anchor, paired with the compact arc of Rotational shadow hook. Ankle Pogo in Place — short bilateral set introduces six consecutive small ankle contacts after the earlier separated jump receptions; these contacts support foot organization and ankle force, rather than adding a running approach. Compared with Class 5, prescribed bilateral foot-landing events change from 3 to 6, low-amplitude foot landings (a subset of foot landings) change from 0 to 6. Review actual recovery and require a free foot-and-hip turn plus small rhythmic ankle receptions with a controlled final stop. If rhythmic landing is unconfirmed, replace only the remaining pogo attempts with the listed grounded quick calf rises and log zero replacement landings. Shorten the rotational step or hand arc when alignment fails, preserving full between-task recovery.

Previous combined Class 5 prescribed 10 primary bouts and 62 conventional/traverse reps plus 0 cycles; this class prescribes 7 bouts, 46 reps and 0 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. This third lesson completes stage coverage and repeats the selected light supports for the stated shoulder/hip/foot jobs. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. The extra light supports are disclosed repeats rather than new work identities. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_06_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_06.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

**Handoff:** Review actual delivery and this saved workload before Class 7; develop its complementary job without assuming these prescriptions were performed.

## Full Body Force Generation — Class 7 of 36

**Stage 3, lesson A: Split-base projection and downward draw**

**Primary effort:** Project from a fixed split base while expressing clear downward and forward arm force.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Split-Stance Broad Jump — fixed lead, two-foot reception as the controlled lower-body force anchor. Project from a fixed split base while expressing clear downward and forward arm force.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Split-Stance Broad Jump — fixed lead, two-foot reception. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Directed force from a fixed base:** Split-Stance Broad Jump — fixed lead, two-foot reception and Medicine Ball Overhead Slam — tall kneeling, arm-driven: Start from the declared support position and direct force into the forward jump or downward arm release; maintain a controlled finish in the chosen line.
- **E3 + E4 — Distal force finish:** Calf Raise — single-leg fast supported finish, no flight and Medicine Ball Chest Pass — stationary, paused: Keep the body organized while force passes through the ankle rise or forward hand release; do not add a leg bounce to the stationary pass.
- **E5 + E6 — Hip initiation into hand force:** Low roundhouse from a held split-squat start and Med Ball Countermovement Rotational Throw — floor target: Upper reversal projection and lower still-start rotational force are distinct tasks; no assumption that one proves mastery of the other.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Split-Stance Broad Jump — fixed lead, two-foot reception** | **2 × 2 per leg** | Main asymmetric leg contribution to a forward push while retaining a bilateral landing. Start in a modest split stance, front whole foot loaded and rear foot assisting. Use one small dip and project a short distance; land with feet parallel and hold 2 s. Repeat with the other lead leg; this is not a single-leg takeoff. Use instead: a shorter split-stance jump, or the Week 1 parallel broad jump if asymmetry loses the forward line; record the changed start. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between complete set rounds; 60 s between leg bouts; 8 total reps; 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E2 | **Medicine Ball Overhead Slam — tall kneeling, arm-driven** | **2 × 3** | Primary rapid shoulder-extension release, pulling the ball down in front of the body. Use a light low-rebound slam ball; tall knees, no sit-back or abdominal crunch. Bring ball to comfortable overhead reach, slam forward/down into the inspected floor zone and let it stop. Use instead: a rapid two-arm Band Straight-Arm Pulldown on a rated overhead attachment if the floor, overhead clearance or rebound is unsuitable; count zero slam releases. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between complete set rounds; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E3 | **Calf Raise — single-leg fast supported finish, no flight** | **1 × 3 per leg** | Reveal each stance ankle’s fast plantar-flexion contribution without balance limiting it. Hold stable support, other foot off the floor; rise quickly on one forefoot, hold 1 s, lower 2 s. Knee stays softly straight and heel aligned; no hop. Use instead: a lightly assisted single-leg rise at the same per-leg dose if the heel path slows or rolls. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 6 total reps; 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E4 | **Medicine Ball Chest Pass — stationary, paused** | **1 × 3** | Retain a small direct forward-release exposure while pulling receives the main investment. Feet planted, hold chest start 1 s and release symmetrically without dip or twist. Use instead: a self-anchored two-hand band speed chest press if containment is unavailable; log the release gap. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E4](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E5 | **Low roundhouse from a held split-squat start** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Link stance-leg force from stillness to the free thigh and pelvic turn. Kicking leg starts rear. Hold a shallow split squat 2 s, then drive through the front support leg into a low roundhouse, actively recoil and return to the original split stance. No initial bounce or deeper dip. A qualified coach must establish the low noncontact execution. Keep the foot below hip height, support foot turning with the pelvis, knee softly extended and recoil active; no target impact, jump, spin or locked-knee snap. Use instead: Make the starting squat shallower instead if rising produces a lurch; do not force a deep hold or fast knee lockout. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 3 · E2](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E6 | **Med Ball Countermovement Rotational Throw — floor target** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Turn one compact preload immediately into release. Stand side-on with a light ball near the rear hip; make one small wind-back then reverse into a two-hand scoop release. Feet may pivot; no entry step or repeated rocking. Hold the finish for 2 seconds. Use a light grippable ball and a verified low target in a permitted, fully contained release area; keep the entire flight, rebound and roll clear of people. Stay grounded, and do not receive a rebound. Use instead: Reduce wind-back range if the athlete pauses, overtwists or loses direction. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 3 · E1](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. Priority horizontal pulling strength: move the load through the shoulder and elbow while keeping the body still. Draw elbow toward hip, hold 1 s, lower 2 s; stop before the shoulder rolls forward or the trunk turns. Use instead: a thigh-supported dumbbell row with the same arm path if a bench does not fit. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s between complete set rounds; 60 s between arm bouts; 24 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| P3 | **Standing Dumbbell Calf Raise — straight knees** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Plantar-flexion force with the knees extended for the final upward/forward push. Use stable hand support if needed; rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s to the floor. Keep knees softly straight and heels tracking over the forefoot. Use instead: a lighter supported standing calf raise; use individual load or leverage that retains the required technical reserve. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P4](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Skull Crusher — floor-supported** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load elbow extension for the finish of a forceful press. Neutral grips, upper arms near vertical; lower 2 s beside the ears, extend under control without shoulder swing. Use instead: a lighter floor-supported elbow extension through comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |

Load controlled internal-rotation torque directly at the hip, complementing the larger leg push used to organise the selected grounded lower turn. Sit on a stable bench with thighs supported and hips and knees near 90 degrees. Fit a secure loop around the lower shins. Keep the other foot planted while the working foot moves a short distance outward, lightly clear of the floor, through hip internal rotation. Keep that knee and the pelvis still. Take 2 seconds outward and 2 seconds returning; do not force end range or twist the knee or ankle. Use instead: Reduce range or band tension for knee, ankle or pelvic movement. If the loop cannot be fitted securely, use self-resisted seated hip internal rotation with the opposite hand resisting the outside of the lower shin instead; manual resistance is a changed and less reproducible load. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 3 · P4](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotation** | **1 × 6 alternating traverses total** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the turning arc and controlled endpoints that transmit lower-body force into the selected upper rotational action. Secure the landmine and qualify the bar, grip and minimum load. Hold the sleeve or suitable grip in both hands with elbows softly bent. Turn the feet and hips with a modest side-to-side loaded arc while keeping ribs over pelvis. Each one-way traverse is one rep; the return is the next counted rep. Take about 2 seconds per traverse and settle briefly at each endpoint. Avoid trunk lean, a trapped knee or an uncontrolled finish. Use instead: Reduce the load or arc for loss of alignment. If a secured, manageable landmine setup cannot be qualified, use a standing two-hand band rotation with a verified side anchor and the same one-way traverse counting instead; log the changed setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** Finish this working bout; no additional set or finisher is prescribed. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 3 · P1](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P4 — Dumbbell Skull Crusher — floor-supported:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P5 — Seated band hip internal rotation:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
- **P6 — Landmine Rotation:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4 alternating one-way traverses total, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

Rotational entry/recovery foot placements belong to complete rotational attempts and are not separately enumerated. Primary: 8 rounds / 13 side-specific bouts, 76 conventional/one-way-traverse reps plus 0 complete walkout cycles (0 heel placements). Preparation: 45 reps/traverses plus 0 walkout cycles, separate from work. Light supports: 22 reps/cycles. These mixed units are prescribed descriptions, not equivalent stress or actual completed work.

**Delivery and timing:** Estimate 75–82 minutes after Access & Prepare 1 with prompt equipment access. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Split-Stance Broad Jump — fixed lead, two-foot reception now practices both fixed lead-foot conditions while retaining two-foot takeoff and reception; Medicine Ball Overhead Slam — tall kneeling, arm-driven adds arm-led downward projection from tall kneeling. The larger number of side bouts reflects explicit lead/arm conditions and the retained source sets, not evidence that an athlete earned more load. Compared with Class 6, prescribed ball releases change from 0 to 13, bilateral foot-landing events change from 6 to 8, explosive set/side bouts change from 9 to 13. Before this longer prescription, check actual recovery and whether the full session can preserve its rests. Require the asymmetric start to keep the forward line and the landing to hold; use a shorter split-start jump or the listed parallel-start broad jump for remaining attempts if it cannot. If the slam setup is unqualified, use the listed rapid two-arm straight-arm pulldown for remaining attempts and record removed releases.

Previous combined Class 6 prescribed 7 primary bouts and 46 conventional/traverse reps plus 0 cycles; this class prescribes 13 bouts, 76 reps and 0 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_07_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_07.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

## Full Body Force Generation — Class 8 of 36

**Stage 3, lesson B: Independent hip and overhead force**

**Primary effort:** Produce force through one hip and an independent overhead arm draw.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Band Lat Pulldown — single-arm rapid elbow drive as the controlled upper-body force anchor. Produce force through one hip and an independent overhead arm draw.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Band Lat Pulldown — single-arm rapid elbow drive. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Independent force with pelvic control:** Band Lat Pulldown — single-arm rapid elbow drive and Glute Bridge — single-leg rapid rise with level pelvis: Keep the pelvis and rib cage organized while one arm draws or one hip extends, avoiding a lateral trunk bend to finish.
- **E3 + E4 — Shoulder initiation and leg-driven release:** Scapular Pull-Up — foot-supported quick initiation and Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — fixed split stance, forward release: Stabilize the trunk so the intended shoulder initiation or leg-to-ball impulse stays clear; preserve the scoop's forward release line.
- **E5 + E6 — Supported rotational force:** Seated light-band hip-rotation acceleration and Rotational band row speed drive: Load the pulling-side turn quickly while directly accelerating hip internal rotation in a supported seated task.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Band Lat Pulldown — single-arm rapid elbow drive** | **1 × 3 per arm** | Coordinate shoulder draw and elbow flexion independently on each side. Kneel below a rated overhead attachment; pull one elbow toward the ribs quickly, return 2 s without trunk side-bending. Use instead: a light supported one-arm dumbbell speed row, 1 × 3 per arm with the same side rest, if the overhead attachment is unsuitable; record the vertical-pull practice gap. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between arm bouts; 6 total reps; 4 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E2 | **Glute Bridge — single-leg rapid rise with level pelvis** | **1 × 2 per leg** | Express hip extension on each side without the second leg hiding pelvic control. One foot grounded, other thigh comfortably lifted; drive hips up quickly, hold 1 s, lower 2 s without twist. Use bodyweight and a short controllable range. Use instead: a kickstand-assisted rapid bridge at the same per-leg dose if the pelvis rotates; record assistance. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 4 total reps; 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E3 | **Scapular Pull-Up — foot-supported quick initiation** | **1 × 4** | Initiate upward body force through shoulder-blade depression before elbow flexion. Feet supported on an inspected box under the bar, elbows straight. Make a small quick shoulder-blade pull to lift the torso slightly, lower 2 s; no shrug bounce or elbow bend. Use instead: a light band straight-arm scapular depression from comfortable overhead reach if bar support is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 4 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E4 | **Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — fixed split stance, forward release** | **1 × 2 per leg** | Carry an asymmetric leg push into a free implement without another landing. Use a light ball between thighs and a small fixed split stance. Hinge slightly, drive through the feet to release forward/upward with long arms. No twist, step or catching return; perform two with each lead leg. Use instead: a parallel-stance hip scoop at the same total four throws if the split base twists; if containment fails, four unloaded rapid hip hinges with zero throws. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E4](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E5 | **Seated light-band hip-rotation acceleration** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Give the hip internal rotators a small rapid force-expression task without the balance or whole-kick demand. Sit with thighs supported, hips/knees near 90 degrees and a very light loop at the lower shins. Start still, then move one foot a short distance outward briskly by rotating at the hip; knee/pelvis stay quiet. Return slowly; no bouncing or forced end range. Use instead: self-resistance from the opposite hand instead if no suitable loop fits, or reduce range/tension for knee movement; speed is controlled, never a ballistic joint wrench. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 3 · E4](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E6 | **Rotational band row speed drive** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Accelerate the pulling side of the turn without jerking the shoulder. Light band anchored ahead at lower-chest height. From a controlled reach, pivot/turn and draw the elbow toward lower ribs quickly; stop cleanly and return in 3 seconds. One pull-return is one rep. Verify the stated band anchor and tension; retain the band throughout, with no release or uncontrolled recoil. Use instead: Reduce tension/reach if the shoulder hikes or the trunk yanks backward. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 3 · E3](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |

| S1 | **Scapular Push-Up — quadruped, dynamic** | **1 × 6** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Lightly load controlled shoulder-blade travel for pushing support and force transfer. Hands and knees down; keep elbows straight. Settle chest 2 s, push away 2 s, hold 2 s without trunk sag. Use instead: a smaller controlled scapular range with more weight supported at knees. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · S2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| S2 | **Side-Lying Hip Abduction — light** | **1 × 5 per leg** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light lateral-hip strengthening to keep the pelvis and knee organized over the force-producing leg. Lie on side, lower leg bent; lift upper leg slightly 2 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s with toes facing forward and pelvis still. Bodyweight is sufficient unless clearly easy. Use instead: a smaller unloaded leg lift with the same per-leg dose. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 10 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · S2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

Hip extension from a lengthened hinge, with the trunk transmitting rather than supplying the motion. Lower 3 s with soft knees, bells close and hips back; stand in about 1 s. Stop range before back rounding or pelvic tuck. Use instead: a lighter two-dumbbell hinge through a shorter controlled range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |

Load the downward arm draw needed for assisted pull-up force without another horizontal row set. Use a manufacturer-permitted band on an inspected overhead anchor; kneel tall, draw elbows to ribs, hold 1 s, return 2 s; no lumbar lean. Use instead: a foot-supported pull-up with comfortable grip and enough assistance to preserve 2–3 RIR; otherwise a supported row with the vertical-pull gap recorded. Each cycle is two small heel steps out per foot, then two back per foot: eight placements in about 8 s. Keep a soft knee and level hips; never chase a fully straight leg. Use instead: a shorter-lever walkout at the same complete-cycle count; reduce range for sagging or cramp and stop if control does not return. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |

Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Band Lat Pulldown — single-arm rapid elbow drive isolates rapid elbow drive in each arm, and Glute Bridge — single-leg rapid rise with level pelvis shifts the grounded hip rise to each leg with a level pelvis. Supported scapular initiation and direct seated hip rotation keep the force jobs explicit while the split-stance scoop retains a small free-release exposure. Compared with Class 7, prescribed ball releases change from 13 to 4, bilateral foot-landing events change from 8 to 0, explosive set/side bouts change from 13 to 11. With actual recovery checked, require the arm draw without side bending and the bridge without pelvic rotation. Use the listed kickstand-assisted bridge for only remaining leg repetitions if the pelvis cannot stay level. If overhead anchoring is unsuitable, use the listed supported one-arm speed row for remaining arm repetitions, preserving side rest and recording the vertical-pull practice gap.

Previous combined Class 7 prescribed 13 primary bouts and 76 conventional/traverse reps plus 0 cycles; this class prescribes 8 bouts, 40 reps and 3 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_08_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_08.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

**Handoff:** Review actual delivery and this saved workload before Class 9; develop its complementary job without assuming these prescriptions were performed.
