## Full Body Force Generation — Class 9 of 36

**Stage 3, lesson C: Lead-leg ascent and traction onset**

**Primary effort:** Drive laterally from a held split stance into an open rotational base and an outward upper-body release. Lead-leg ascent, a short linear start and arm draws reinforce support and the pulling finish.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Lateral release step from held split stance as the grounded rotational force anchor. Release planted leg force into a grounded turn when there is no incoming momentum.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Lateral release step from held split stance. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Turn and control the finish:** Lateral release step from held split stance and Two-hand medicine-ball backhand throw: Outward upper sweep paired with a held-start lateral leg push that opens into a rotational stance.
- **E3 + E4 — Supported onset and controlled reversal:** Step-Up to Knee Drive — fast ascent, no flight and Band Row — controlled reach into immediate fast reversal: Use stable support to organize the lead-leg drive or loaded arm reversal, then finish without borrowing a torso rock.
- **E5 + E6 — Short force impulse and reset:** Standing Split-Start Acceleration — 2 m, full stop and Band Biceps Curl — rapid neutral-grip pull: Produce the short forward start or elbow-flexion impulse from an organized base, then regain control before the next attempt.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Lateral release step from held split stance** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Release planted leg force into a grounded turn when there is no incoming momentum. Hold a shallow split stance for 2 s with the named leg in front. Push from that leg into one short lateral step, allow the feet/pelvis to open together up to 45 degrees, and finish in a stable kick stance. No jump or follow-on kick. Let the support foot or both feet turn freely with the pelvis; never force rotation through a trapped knee. Remain grounded. Use instead: Shorten the step and turn for foot sticking or overstriding; keep motion within 2 m. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 3 · E6](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E2 | **Two-hand medicine-ball backhand throw** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Express outward transverse force without relying on elbow extension. Begin hands across the front of the body near the opposite lower ribs, elbows softly bent. Turn toward the cleared lane and sweep both hands outward at waist-to-chest height into the low target. Reset without receiving a rebound. Use a light grippable ball and a verified low target in a permitted, fully contained release area; keep the entire flight, rebound and roll clear of people. Stay grounded, and do not receive a rebound. Use instead: Shorten the sweep if the ball drags the shoulder behind the torso. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 3 · E5](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E3 | **Step-Up to Knee Drive — fast ascent, no flight** | **1 × 2 per leg** | Coordinate lead-leg extension with free-leg recovery while keeping the support foot planted. Use a low immovable step, lead whole foot planted. Rise quickly and bring the trailing knee forward to hip level only if balanced; hold 1 s, lower 3 s. No launch or jump from the top. Use instead: the Week 1 rapid step-up with the rear foot joining the support at the same per-leg dose when free-leg balance is unconfirmed. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 4 total reps; 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E3](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E4 | **Band Row — controlled reach into immediate fast reversal** | **1 × 4** | Reverse an actively controlled forward reach into backward force without an inertial pause. Use two-hand seated permitted foot anchoring. Reach forward for 2 s under tension, immediately draw to ribs, then rest in the finish before the next cycle; no torso rock. Use instead: a paused seated fast band row if reversal timing cannot be controlled; identify the repeated Class 1 execution. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 5 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E5 | **Standing Split-Start Acceleration — 2 m, full stop** | **1 × 1 per leg** | Apply a lead-leg push to the first running steps and then regain control. Begin stationary in a comfortable split stance; push forward and accelerate for only 2 m, then decelerate within the final inspected 7 m after reserving 1 m for the start stance. One attempt with each lead leg. No jump start, fall or backward rock; use submaximal effort if needed to stop comfortably. Use instead: two fast grounded supported split-squat rises total, one per lead leg, when the running/stopping envelope is unsuitable; record zero sprint metres. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between leg bouts; 2 total reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| E6 | **Band Biceps Curl — rapid neutral-grip pull** | **1 × 4** | Express elbow-flexion force quickly without shoulder or hip assistance masking it. Stand on a manufacturer-suitable band secured beneath both midfeet; palms face inward, elbows at ribs. Flex quickly, lower 2 s; stop before wrists or shoulders substitute. Use instead: a very light simultaneous dumbbell hammer curl with rapid controlled ascent if the band/grip setup is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 4 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · E5](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Scapular Push-Up — quadruped, dynamic** | **1 × 6** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Lightly load controlled shoulder-blade travel for pushing support and force transfer. Hands and knees down; keep elbows straight. Settle chest 2 s, push away 2 s, hold 2 s without trunk sag. Use instead: a smaller controlled scapular range with more weight supported at knees. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · S2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| S2 | **Side-Lying Hip Abduction — light** | **1 × 5 per leg** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light lateral-hip strengthening to keep the pelvis and knee organized over the force-producing leg. Lie on side, lower leg bent; lift upper leg slightly 2 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s with toes facing forward and pelvis still. Bodyweight is sufficient unless clearly easy. Use instead: a smaller unloaded leg lift with the same per-leg dose. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 10 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · S2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Goblet Squat — dumbbell** | **1 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Bilateral knee/hip extension through a repeatable full-foot push. Lower 3 s, stand decisively in about 1 s; hold a manageable goblet load and retain comfortable depth without heels lifting. Use instead: a lighter goblet squat through controlled comfortable range; a two-dumbbell front hold may replace a limiting goblet grip. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P3](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| P2 | **Dumbbell Rear-Delt Row — bilateral, hinged** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Strengthen the wider-elbow backward draw that controls the arm-open position. Hold a still hinge; elbows about 60 degrees from torso, pull to upper ribs, hold 1 s, lower 2 s. Use instead: a chest-supported version only on an inspected bench with adequate arm clearance. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| P3 | **Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Plantar-flexion force while the knee remains flexed during early push and receiving. Sit on an inspected stable bench, feet on floor; secure loads on thighs, rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s without knee-angle change or bouncing. Use instead: a supported standing bent-knee calf raise with the same bilateral dose and 2–3 technical RIR if bench/load support is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Hammer Curl — simultaneous arms** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load elbow flexors for finishing the backward draw without another shoulder-extension set. Upper arms stay at ribs; lift 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s, without hip swing or wrist collapse. Use instead: a lighter neutral-grip curl without body movement. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 3 · P4](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_03.md). |
| P5 | **Loaded Cossack Squat** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a substantial lateral knee-and-hip compound, strengthening weight acceptance and return over the stance leg that supports the selected lower rotational action. Hold a suitable dumbbell at the chest in a comfortable wide stance. Sit toward the working hip as the other leg lengthens, keeping the loaded foot supported and the knee following the toes. Return to centre under control. Use about 2 seconds lowering, 1 second lifting and 1 second organising the next rep. Depth is individually qualified; do not force a deep groin stretch or turn on a fixed knee. Use instead: a shallower controlled Cossack range and adjust the dumbbell load for knee or pelvic-position faults. Stop for groin pain or inability to qualify a challenging controlled range; do not add a second movement. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 3 · P3](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotational Press** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a compound unilateral push for the full-body force work while loading the connection from a freely turning foot and hip to a controlled arm press. Its diagonal pressing path complements the selected rotational task; it does not reproduce every throw path. Secure the landmine and qualify its bar, grip and minimum load. In a modest staggered stance, hold the sleeve near the rear shoulder with the rear hand. Turn the rear foot and hips into a controlled forward-and-up press, keeping wrist stacked and ribs level. No dip-drive, jump or release. Return in 3 seconds and reset without shoulder shrug or lower-back arch. Use instead: Reduce load or range for wrist, elbow or rib-position faults. If the minimum landmine setup is unsuitable, use a one-arm rotational band press from a verified anchor instead; log the changed load and setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 3 · P3](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_03.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

## Full Body Force Generation — Class 10 of 36

**Stage 4, lesson A: Held hand push and vertical leg drive**

**Primary effort:** Express a settled arm push and a direct upward leg impulse.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Countermovement Jump — vertical rise and stick as the controlled lower-body force anchor. Express a settled arm push and a direct upward leg impulse.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Countermovement Jump — vertical rise and stick. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Controlled start into projection:** Countermovement Jump — vertical rise and stick and Plyo Push-Up — paused bottom start, hands elevated: Organize the start before the rapid impulse and accept the hand or foot reception under control; keep each task's prescribed preload distinct.
- **E3 + E4 — Force against a retained load:** Seated Bent-Knee Calf Raise — fast light rise and Two-Hand Landmine Bent-Over Row — fast pull from a pause: Stabilize the support base while the ankle or arms accelerate the retained load, then lower under control without an extra body lift.
- **E5 + E6 — Hip initiation into hand force:** Low roundhouse to suspended re-chamber and Step-in rotational medicine-ball shot-put: Step-in upper push and controlled lower kick recoil organize an active finish.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Countermovement Jump — vertical rise and stick** | **2 × 3** | Main coordinated knee/hip push after one controlled countermovement. Use one comfortable dip with arm swing, jump vertically, receive on both feet and hold 2 s. No repeated bouncing, deep-collapse dip or knee tuck for apparent height. Use instead: a smaller countermovement jump or fast nonflight squat rise when reception cannot stay controlled; record removed landings. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between complete set rounds; 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · E1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| E2 | **Plyo Push-Up — paused bottom start, hands elevated** | **2 × 3** | Primary fast push from a still loaded position rather than an immediate lowering-to-push rebound. On a stable elevated support, lower to a controlled bottom, hold 2 s without relaxing, then push into a small hand flight and soft return. Reset fully; no clap. Use instead: a fast non-flight incline push-up from the same controlled pause when hand reception is unsuitable; record zero hand landings. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between complete set rounds; 12 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · E1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| E3 | **Seated Bent-Knee Calf Raise — fast light rise** | **1 × 4** | Express rapid plantar-flexion force with the knee held flexed. Sit securely, feet on floor, very light controlled thigh loads if needed; lift heels quickly, hold 1 s and lower 2 s. Keep knee angle fixed; no bouncing or hand pull on the loads. Use instead: a supported standing bent-knee rapid heel rise at the same bilateral dose if the seated setup fails. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · E4](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| E4 | **Two-Hand Landmine Bent-Over Row — fast pull from a pause** | **1 × 3** | Retain one low-volume backward force task so the pressing day still includes deliberate pulling. Light load; still hinge, pull sleeve toward lower ribs quickly, lower 2 s, pause extended arms without hip drive. Use instead: a light two-hand kettlebell paused row if the landmine cannot be handled quickly. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 4 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · E6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| E5 | **Low roundhouse to suspended re-chamber** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Directly train the rapid return and controlled arrest of the kicking leg, rather than allowing it to fall after extension. From a stationary rear-leg kick stance, perform one low roundhouse. Immediately fold the knee and recover the thigh to a low chamber; hold the foot off the floor for 2 s before placing it back in stance. The athlete does not jump. A qualified coach must establish the low noncontact execution. Keep the foot below hip height, support foot turning with the pelvis, knee softly extended and recoil active; no target impact, jump, spin or locked-knee snap. Use instead: a shorter extension/low chamber instead if the foot drops or the support knee twists; hold quality matters more than range. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 4 · E2](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_04.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E6 | **Step-in rotational medicine-ball shot-put** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Turn a deliberate plant into a directed pushing release. Start staggered with ball at the rear shoulder; take one small lead-foot step toward the low forward target, settle that foot, pivot the rear hip and press the ball with the rear hand. Hold the finish. Use a light grippable ball and a verified low target in a permitted, fully contained release area; keep the entire flight, rebound and roll clear of people. Stay grounded, and do not receive a rebound. Keep all entry and finish footwork inside a cleared 1-metre start bay; no hop, jump or sprint. Use instead: the standing rotational shot-put instead at the same dose if a step causes falling or knee twist. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 4 · E1](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_04.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Supine Dumbbell Serratus Reach — elbows straight** | **1 × 8** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Lightly load scapular reach at the press finish while the floor supports trunk position. Very light paired dumbbells above chest; keep elbows straight, reach shoulder blades slightly off floor 2 s, hold 1 s, return 2 s without rolling the trunk. Use instead: an unloaded supine reach through the same comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · S1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| S2 | **Tibialis Raise — wall-supported** | **1 × 6** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light ankle dorsiflexor loading to control foot position before a stable push/landing. Back against an inspected wall, heels on floor; lift forefeet 2 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s. Adjust foot distance to stay easy. Use instead: a seated unweighted toe raise through comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · S1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Goblet Squat — dumbbell** | **2 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Bilateral knee/hip extension through a repeatable full-foot push. Lower 3 s, stand decisively in about 1 s; hold a manageable goblet load and retain comfortable depth without heels lifting. Use instead: a lighter goblet squat through controlled comfortable range; a two-dumbbell front hold may replace a limiting goblet grip. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s between complete set rounds; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · P1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| P2 | **Floor Press — two dumbbells, neutral grip** | **2 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Main horizontal pressing force with a reproducible floor-limited range. Lower 2 s, touch upper arms gently, pause 1 s, then press decisively; feet stay down. Coach supervises pickup/set-down. Use instead: a lighter floor press through controlled comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 150 s between complete set rounds; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · P1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| P3 | **Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Plantar-flexion force while the knee remains flexed during early push and receiving. Sit on an inspected stable bench, feet on floor; secure loads on thighs, rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s without knee-angle change or bouncing. Use instead: a supported standing bent-knee calf raise with the same bilateral dose and 2–3 technical RIR if bench/load support is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · P5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Rear-Delt Row — bilateral, hinged** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Strengthen the wider-elbow backward draw that controls the arm-open position. Hold a still hinge; elbows about 60 degrees from torso, pull to upper ribs, hold 1 s, lower 2 s. Use instead: a chest-supported version only on an inspected bench with adequate arm clearance. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · P4](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| P5 | **Standing Cable or Band Hip Flexion** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Challenge active chamber lifting and controlled lowering so the free leg can accelerate and recover while the other leg supplies the rotational base. Stand upright with a secure band loop at the forefeet, the support foot anchoring its end and optional light fingertip contact on a stable support. Raise the bent knee to a comfortable low chamber with the pelvis quiet, then lower without swinging. Use about 1 second lifting, 2 seconds lowering and 1 second resetting. Do not use momentum, a hop or an unsecured loop. Use instead: Reduce tension or chamber height for pelvic tipping or loss of foot security. If the loop cannot be secured, use self-resisted seated bent-knee hip flexion with the hands resisting the distal thigh instead; record the changed, less reproducible resistance. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 4 · P5](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_04.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotational Press** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a compound unilateral push for the full-body force work while loading the connection from a freely turning foot and hip to a controlled arm press. Its diagonal pressing path complements the selected rotational task; it does not reproduce every throw path. Secure the landmine and qualify its bar, grip and minimum load. In a modest staggered stance, hold the sleeve near the rear shoulder with the rear hand. Turn the rear foot and hips into a controlled forward-and-up press, keeping wrist stacked and ribs level. No dip-drive, jump or release. Return in 3 seconds and reset without shoulder shrug or lower-back arch. Use instead: Reduce load or range for wrist, elbow or rib-position faults. If the minimum landmine setup is unsuitable, use a one-arm rotational band press from a verified anchor instead; log the changed load and setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 4 · P1](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_04.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

**Preparation sets, separate from working:**

- **P1 — Goblet Squat — dumbbell:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 5 then 1 × 3; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 90 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P2 — Floor Press — two dumbbells, neutral grip:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 6 then 1 × 3. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 90 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P3 — Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P4 — Dumbbell Rear-Delt Row — bilateral, hinged:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P5 — Standing Cable or Band Hip Flexion:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
- **P6 — Landmine Rotational Press:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

**Workload:** 0 nonrotational and 4 rotational ball releases, including 0 nonrotational downward slams and 0 rotational slams already included in those release totals; 0 source ball-receiving events and 0 rotational handoffs counted separately. 6 bilateral / 0 unilateral foot-landings (12 individual landing-foot contacts), with 0 low-amplitude events already included; 0 unilateral takeoffs. 6 bilateral hand-receptions / 12 hand contacts. 4 grounded rapid leg reps, 4 grounded lower-rotation efforts (including 4 kick actions) and 0 retained/unloaded upper-rotation efforts. 0 short starts / 0 accelerating metres; 0 counted lower source-force walking-entry steps and 0 counted upper source-force task steps. Rotational entry/recovery foot placements belong to complete rotational attempts and are not separately enumerated. Primary: 8 rounds / 10 side-specific bouts, 60 conventional/one-way-traverse reps plus 0 complete walkout cycles (0 heel placements). Preparation: 38 reps/traverses plus 0 walkout cycles, separate from work. Light supports: 14 reps/cycles. These mixed units are prescribed descriptions, not equivalent stress or actual completed work.

**Delivery and timing:** Estimate 66–73 minutes after Access & Prepare 1 with prompt equipment access. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Countermovement Jump — vertical rise and stick uses a controlled countermovement for upward force, while Plyo Push-Up — paused bottom start, hands elevated revisits elevated hand flight with a held bottom start instead of an immediate reversal. Separate foot and hand receiving tasks therefore return in the same class; the kick and rotational throw remain grounded, and the stations are not combined. Compared with Class 9, prescribed bilateral foot-landing events change from 0 to 6, bilateral hand-receiving events change from 0 to 6. Proceed only with reviewed recovery and independently qualified foot and hand reception; a successful jump does not qualify the hand-flight task. Use the listed smaller jump or nonflight squat rise for remaining E1 work and the held-start nonflight incline push-up for remaining E2 work when reception is unsuitable. Record each removed event type and retain the full set and between-task rests.

Previous combined Class 9 prescribed 8 primary bouts and 50 conventional/traverse reps plus 0 cycles; this class prescribes 10 bouts, 60 reps and 0 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_10_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_10.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

**Handoff:** Review actual delivery and this saved workload before Class 11; develop its complementary job without assuming these prescriptions were performed.

## Full Body Force Generation — Class 11 of 36

**Stage 4, lesson B: Short reception to controlled release**

**Primary effort:** Receive a small load or landing, settle it, and produce the next clear force action.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Band Triceps Pressdown — rapid elbow extension as the controlled upper-body force anchor. Receive a small load or landing, settle it, and produce the next clear force action.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Band Triceps Pressdown — rapid elbow extension. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Finish force and regain support:** Band Triceps Pressdown — rapid elbow extension and Box Jump — low target, stand and step down: Complete the elbow extension or upward leg drive decisively, then regain a quiet supported position; the box return remains a step down.
- **E3 + E4 — Set the load before the next impulse:** Medicine Ball Catch-Set Chest Pass — gentle coach feed and Kettlebell Swing — dead-stop two-hand repetitions: Control the received ball or parked kettlebell before producing the next clear force action; use the assigned reset rather than an uncontrolled continuous cycle.
- **E5 + E6 — Supported rotational force:** Low inward crescent shadow sweep and Light-band rotational uppercut: Rising hand arc and inward low-leg arc use complementary rotational paths.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Band Triceps Pressdown — rapid elbow extension** | **1 × 4** | Isolate quick elbow extension for the finish of the pressing action. Band on an inspected rated overhead attachment; elbows stay at ribs. Extend quickly without slamming lockout, return 2 s and keep shoulders still. Use instead: a very light floor-supported dumbbell elbow extension with decisive controlled ascent if the band anchor is unavailable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 4 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · E2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| E2 | **Box Jump — low target, stand and step down** | **1 × 3** | Direct knee-led upward force to a clear receiving surface without a drop rebound. Jump from floor to a low inspected immovable box; choose height permitting modest knee lift and a quiet full-foot landing. Stand tall, then step down under control; no jump down or rebound. Use instead: three settled-start squat jumps with hands at hips: hold a quarter squat 2 s, jump without a second dip and land on both feet for a 2 s hold, using the same 10 s resets; this static-start task differs from the source exercise “Countermovement Jump — vertical rise and stick” countermovement. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · E2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| E3 | **Medicine Ball Catch-Set Chest Pass — gentle coach feed** | **1 × 3** | Accept a small incoming load, stabilize it, then create forward force from a settled chest position. A coach gives a soft two-hand feed at chest height from a short inspected distance. Receive with yielding elbows, hold still 1 s, then pass forward; no immediate rebound chain. Use only a ball the athlete can comfortably receive. Use instead: a self-started stationary chest pass if feed control or receiving readiness is absent; record zero catches, and use a self-anchored band press if release containment also fails. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · E3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| E4 | **Kettlebell Swing — dead-stop two-hand repetitions** | **1 × 3** | Rapid hip extension moves a retained implement without adding landings. Hike a light kettlebell from a stable parked start, snap hips to a tall finish with a chest-level float, guide the return and park fully after each rep. No squat pull, arm lift or lumbar arch. Use instead: a very light rapid dumbbell hip hinge with a 2 s lowering and still finish if swing skill or grip is unconfirmed. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · E6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| E5 | **Low inward crescent shadow sweep** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Express an inward thigh/adductor sweep that helps recover the leg after rotational extension. With a soft knee, sweep the low free foot from slightly outside toward the midline in a small inward arc, coordinated with a comfortable support-foot/pelvic turn. Finish by folding the knee and placing the foot down. No crossing beyond a controllable midline range. A qualified coach must establish the low noncontact execution. Keep the foot below hip height, support foot turning with the pelvis, knee softly extended and recoil active; no target impact, jump, spin or locked-knee snap. Use instead: a bent-knee inward sweep instead at the same dose if the longer lever pulls at the groin; never force a high or locked-knee crescent. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 4 · E4](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_04.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E6 | **Light-band rotational uppercut** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Apply the turn to an upward hand path against retained resistance. Band secured low behind the working side, hand near the hip. With bent elbow, pivot the rear foot and drive the fist toward upper-chest height; stop below shoulder shrug and return slowly. Verify the stated band anchor and tension; retain the band throughout, with no release or uncontrolled recoil. Use instead: Reduce tension/height if the trunk arches or band pulls the wrist out of line. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 4 · E2](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_04.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| S1 | **Bilateral Band External Rotation — elbows at sides** | **1 × 8** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light shoulder-rotator strengthening to control upper-arm position during the day's press and pull. Unanchored band between hands, elbows at ribs; rotate outward 2 s, hold 1 s, return 2 s without shrugging. Use instead: a lower-tension band and shorter comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 10 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · S2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Romanian Deadlift — two dumbbells** | **1 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Hip extension from a lengthened hinge, with the trunk transmitting rather than supplying the motion. Lower 3 s with soft knees, bells close and hips back; stand in about 1 s. Stop range before back rounding or pelvic tuck. Use instead: a lighter two-dumbbell hinge through a shorter controlled range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · P2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| P2 | **One-Arm Row — dumbbell, hand and knee on bench** | **1 × 6 per arm** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the arm-driven backward draw while support prevents torso rotation. Draw elbow toward hip, hold 1 s, lower 2 s; stop before the shoulder rolls forward or the trunk turns. Use instead: a thigh-supported dumbbell row with the same arm path if a bench does not fit. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between arm bouts; 12 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · P2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| P3 | **Hamstring Walkout — complete out-and-back cycle** | **1 × 3 cycles** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Knee-flexor leverage while the pelvis stays raised, distinct from moving through a hip hinge. Start in a bilateral bridge. Each cycle is two small heel steps out per foot, then two back per foot: eight placements in about 8 s. Keep a soft knee and level hips; never chase a fully straight leg. Use instead: a shorter-lever walkout at the same complete-cycle count; reduce range for sagging or cramp and stop if control does not return. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · P6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Skull Crusher — floor-supported** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load elbow extension for the finish of a forceful press. Neutral grips, upper arms near vertical; lower 2 s beside the ears, extend under control without shoulder swing. Use instead: a lighter floor-supported elbow extension through comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 4 · P5](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_04.md). |
| P5 | **Loaded Cossack Squat** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a substantial lateral knee-and-hip compound, strengthening weight acceptance and return over the stance leg that supports the selected lower rotational action. Hold a suitable dumbbell at the chest in a comfortable wide stance. Sit toward the working hip as the other leg lengthens, keeping the loaded foot supported and the knee following the toes. Return to centre under control. Use about 2 seconds lowering, 1 second lifting and 1 second organising the next rep. Depth is individually qualified; do not force a deep groin stretch or turn on a fixed knee. Use instead: a shallower controlled Cossack range and adjust the dumbbell load for knee or pelvic-position faults. Stop for groin pain or inability to qualify a challenging controlled range; do not add a second movement. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 4 · P3](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_04.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Cable / Band Lift — standing band execution** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the low-to-high turning path from the hip to the hands and control its return, supporting the selected rising or scooping upper rotational action. Secure a band at a verified low anchor behind one side. Stand in a comfortable split stance. Guide both hands from the rear hip across toward the opposite upper chest, allowing a small foot-and-hip turn. Drive in 1–2 seconds and return in 3 seconds. Keep ribs stacked and stop below any range that requires leaning back or shrugging. No cable machine is assumed. Use instead: Reduce resistance or range if the athlete leans back or loses a controlled return. If a suitable secure low anchor is unavailable, stop and re-audit the slot; do not improvise an unsafe anchor. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 4 · P6](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_04.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

**Preparation sets, separate from working:**

- **P1 — Romanian Deadlift — two dumbbells:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 5; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 90 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P2 — One-Arm Row — dumbbell, hand and knee on bench:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5 per arm. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, 30 seconds between arms, then 90 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P3 — Hamstring Walkout — complete out-and-back cycle:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 1 cycles; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P4 — Dumbbell Skull Crusher — floor-supported:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P5 — Loaded Cossack Squat:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
- **P6 — Cable / Band Lift — standing band execution:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

**Workload:** 3 nonrotational and 0 rotational ball releases, including 0 nonrotational downward slams and 0 rotational slams already included in those release totals; 3 source ball-receiving events and 0 rotational handoffs counted separately. 3 bilateral / 0 unilateral foot-landings (6 individual landing-foot contacts), with 0 low-amplitude events already included; 0 unilateral takeoffs. 0 bilateral hand-receptions / 0 hand contacts. 3 grounded rapid leg reps, 4 grounded lower-rotation efforts (including 4 kick actions) and 4 retained/unloaded upper-rotation efforts. 0 short starts / 0 accelerating metres; 0 counted lower source-force walking-entry steps and 0 counted upper source-force task steps. Rotational entry/recovery foot placements belong to complete rotational attempts and are not separately enumerated. Primary: 6 rounds / 9 side-specific bouts, 46 conventional/one-way-traverse reps plus 3 complete walkout cycles (24 heel placements). Preparation: 32 reps/traverses plus 1 walkout cycles, separate from work. Light supports: 18 reps/cycles. These mixed units are prescribed descriptions, not equivalent stress or actual completed work.

**Delivery and timing:** Estimate 59–66 minutes after Access & Prepare 1 with prompt equipment access. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Band Triceps Pressdown — rapid elbow extension gives elbow extension a small rapid finishing job while Box Jump — low target, stand and step down places a low target under the leg projection. Medicine Ball Catch-Set Chest Pass — gentle coach feed introduces a gentle external ball feed followed by a deliberate set before release, making receiving control distinct from an immediate rebound. Compared with Class 10, prescribed ball releases change from 4 to 3, bilateral foot-landing events change from 6 to 3, bilateral hand-receiving events change from 6 to 0, airborne feed catches change from 0 to 3. Require actual recovery, a box reception that can be stood up from before stepping down, and a feed that can be received quietly and set. If feed control or receiving readiness is absent, use the listed self-started stationary chest pass only for remaining E3 attempts and record zero replacement catches. If the box is unsuitable, use the listed settled-start floor jump at the same remaining dose and resets.

Previous combined Class 10 prescribed 10 primary bouts and 60 conventional/traverse reps plus 0 cycles; this class prescribes 9 bouts, 46 reps and 3 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_11_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_11.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

**Handoff:** Review actual delivery and this saved workload before Class 12; develop its complementary job without assuming these prescriptions were performed.
