Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Vertical Jump — two linked rises, final stick moves the two-jump connection from the earlier forward projections to vertical rises. Medicine Ball Receive-Step Chest Pass — one controlled plant integrates a gentle receive, one controlled plant and release, while Handoff-to-step rotational medicine-ball scoop return adds a secure handoff before a separate step-to-rotation throw; the four handoffs remain distinct from the four airborne feed catches. Compared with Class 30, prescribed ball releases change from 3 to 8, airborne feed catches change from 3 to 4, secure handoffs change from 0 to 4. With actual recovery reviewed, require the first jump reception to organize the second takeoff and each ball supply to be secure before stepping. Use separated countermovement jumps only for remaining E1 attempts with the listed 15-second resets if the link fails; use the stationary catch-set or self-started pass for remaining E2 attempts as needed. Use a self-held step-in scoop for remaining E6 attempts when handoff timing or feeder clearance fails; log changed catches, handoffs and time.

Previous combined Class 30 prescribed 7 primary bouts and 37 conventional/traverse reps plus 2 cycles; this class prescribes 9 bouts, 51 reps and 0 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. ## Full Body Force Generation — Class 32 of 36

**Stage 11, lesson B: Cued arm force and grounded leg reversal**

**Primary effort:** Express force from a clear cue or controlled reversal while retaining grounded support.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Single-Arm Band Chest Press — still start on a spoken cue as the controlled upper-body force anchor. Express force from a clear cue or controlled reversal while retaining grounded support.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Single-Arm Band Chest Press — still start on a spoken cue. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Controlled reversal and clear cue:** Single-Arm Band Chest Press — still start on a spoken cue and Step-Down to Step-Up — quick grounded reversal: Keep the support position stable as the step reverses or the cued arm presses, with the intended start clear before accelerating.
- **E3 + E4 — Grounded force with a tall finish:** Landmine Power Row — continuous hip-to-arm drive and Glute Bridge — fast grounded rise, settled floor start: Use the deliberate hip impulse to produce the arm draw or bridge rise, finishing through the hips without arching the back.
- **E5 + E6 — Supported rotational force:** Short drop-step lead-leg roundhouse and Light-band rotational cross: Retained upper cross keeps setup familiar while the lower lead-leg kick uses a short support-hip loading step.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Single-Arm Band Chest Press — still start on a spoken cue** | **1 × 2 per arm** | Start an independent forward force pulse without turning or anticipating the cue. Self-anchored band across upper back, other hand secures the tail at torso; coach gives one simple cue after 2–4 s. Press quickly, return 2 s and fully reset. Use instead: a self-initiated single-arm band speed press if the cue disrupts posture. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between arm bouts; 4 total reps; 5 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · E2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| E2 | **Step-Down to Step-Up — quick grounded reversal** | **1 × 1 per leg** | Accept a small controlled lowering then push through the lead foot without flight. Stand with both feet on a low inspected step. Keep the lead foot planted, lower the other heel to a light floor touch in 2 s, then drive back up quickly until both feet join. One complete down-and-up rep per lead leg. Do not bounce from the floor foot. Use instead: a shallow supported single-leg squat lower-and-rise at the same per-leg dose if the step height or path is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 2 total reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · E3](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| E3 | **Landmine Power Row — continuous hip-to-arm drive** | **1 × 3** | Integrate a small body impulse into arm pulling while keeping the load path controlled. Short hinge, light sleeve grip; connect a small hip extension to elbow draw in one pull, then return slowly. No separate deadlift, jump or release. Use instead: the strict paused landmine row if connected timing is not controlled. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 8 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · E4](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| E4 | **Glute Bridge — fast grounded rise, settled floor start** | **1 × 3** | Express hip extension near its finish without another lengthened hinge or landing. Bodyweight/light secured pelvic load; settle hips gently on floor, drive up quickly, hold 1 s, lower 2 s. Keep feet down and knees still; finish without back arch. Use instead: an unloaded smaller-range fast bridge if load handling or pelvic control is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · E6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| E5 | **Short drop-step lead-leg roundhouse** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Use a compact rear-hip load to free the lead leg while retaining the planned kick line. The kicking leg begins in front. Take one small rear-foot drop step to load the support hip, turn that foot freely and deliver a low lead-leg roundhouse without another gather. Recoil into a comfortable split stance; stay within 2 m. A qualified coach must establish the low noncontact execution. Keep the foot below hip height, support foot turning with the pelvis, knee softly extended and recoil active; no target impact, jump, spin or locked-knee snap. Use instead: a shallower drop step instead if the lead leg drags or the support knee twists; do not add speed and step length together. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 11 · E4](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_11.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E6 | **Light-band rotational cross** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Retain a predictable turn-to-hand resisted reference. Band behind rear shoulder; pivot the rear foot/hip and drive the hand forward, stopping short of elbow lock. Return slowly before another rep. Verify the stated band anchor and tension; retain the band throughout, with no release or uncontrolled recoil. Use instead: Reduce tension/reach if wrist or rib position changes. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 11 · E4](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_11.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Dumbbell Wrist Extension — forearm supported** | **1 × 6 per arm** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Lightly support wrist alignment for the received ball, grip and hand-support finish. Forearm supported palm down, very light load; lift 1 s, lower 3 s without forcing range. Use instead: unloaded wrist extension if the lightest implement prevents control. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between arm bouts; 12 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · S2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| S2 | **Side-Lying Hip Adduction — light** | **1 × 4 per leg** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light medial-hip force to maintain a straight stance-leg push without drifting away from the foot. Top foot rests in front of the lower knee. Lift the straight lower leg slightly 2 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s; pelvis remains stacked. Use instead: a smaller controlled lower-leg lift at the same dose. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 8 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · S2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Romanian Deadlift — two dumbbells** | **1 × 5** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Hip extension from a lengthened hinge, with the trunk transmitting rather than supplying the motion. Lower 3 s with soft knees, bells close and hips back; stand in about 1 s. Stop range before back rounding or pelvic tuck. Use instead: a lighter two-dumbbell hinge through a shorter controlled range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · P2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| P2 | **One-Arm Row — dumbbell, hand and knee on bench** | **2 × 6 per arm** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the arm-driven backward draw while support prevents torso rotation. Draw elbow toward hip, hold 1 s, lower 2 s; stop before the shoulder rolls forward or the trunk turns. Use instead: a thigh-supported dumbbell row with the same arm path if a bench does not fit. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s between complete set rounds; 60 s between arm bouts; 24 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · P2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| P3 | **Hamstring Walkout — complete out-and-back cycle** | **1 × 2 cycles** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Knee-flexor leverage while the pelvis stays raised, distinct from moving through a hip hinge. Start in a bilateral bridge. Each cycle is two small heel steps out per foot, then two back per foot: eight placements in about 8 s. Keep a soft knee and level hips; never chase a fully straight leg. Use instead: a shorter-lever walkout at the same complete-cycle count; reduce range for sagging or cramp and stop if control does not return. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · P6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Skull Crusher — floor-supported** | **1 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load elbow extension for the finish of a forceful press. Neutral grips, upper arms near vertical; lower 2 s beside the ears, extend under control without shoulder swing. Use instead: a lighter floor-supported elbow extension through comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · P5](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| P5 | **Loaded Cossack Squat** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a substantial lateral knee-and-hip compound, strengthening weight acceptance and return over the stance leg that supports the selected lower rotational action. Hold a suitable dumbbell at the chest in a comfortable wide stance. Sit toward the working hip as the other leg lengthens, keeping the loaded foot supported and the knee following the toes. Return to centre under control. Use about 2 seconds lowering, 1 second lifting and 1 second organising the next rep. Depth is individually qualified; do not force a deep groin stretch or turn on a fixed knee. Use instead: a shallower controlled Cossack range and adjust the dumbbell load for knee or pelvic-position faults. Stop for groin pain or inability to qualify a challenging controlled range; do not add a second movement. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 11 · P3](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_11.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotational Press** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a compound unilateral push for the full-body force work while loading the connection from a freely turning foot and hip to a controlled arm press. Its diagonal pressing path complements the selected rotational task; it does not reproduce every throw path. Secure the landmine and qualify its bar, grip and minimum load. In a modest staggered stance, hold the sleeve near the rear shoulder with the rear hand. Turn the rear foot and hips into a controlled forward-and-up press, keeping wrist stacked and ribs level. No dip-drive, jump or release. Return in 3 seconds and reset without shoulder shrug or lower-back arch. Use instead: Reduce load or range for wrist, elbow or rib-position faults. If the minimum landmine setup is unsuitable, use a one-arm rotational band press from a verified anchor instead; log the changed load and setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 11 · P2](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_11.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

**Preparation sets, separate from working:**

- **P1 — Romanian Deadlift — two dumbbells:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 5; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 90 s before working sets. - **P5 — Loaded Cossack Squat:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

## Full Body Force Generation — Class 33 of 36

**Stage 11, lesson C: Brief contact to forward projection**

**Primary effort:** Place crossover recovery into a controlled rotational base and couple a separate step-in hip turn to an upper-body pushing release. Small linear leg, ankle and arm-force tasks support placement and the finish.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Crossover recovery to a marked kick base as the grounded rotational force anchor. Recover an exact useful two-foot base after reorientation, instead of leaving the feet crossed or too narrow.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Crossover recovery to a marked kick base. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Turn and control the finish:** Crossover recovery to a marked kick base and Step-in rotational medicine-ball shot-put: A familiar upper step-in press pairs with grounded crossover recovery to a marked kick base; no added jump contacts.
- **E3 + E4 — Directed forward release:** Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — fixed split stance, forward release and Medicine Ball Overhead Forward Throw — fixed stance, arm-driven: Keep the release line and body finish organized; use the scoop's leg drive and the overhead throw's arm action as distinct force routes.
- **E5 + E6 — Brief contact and controlled reception:** Ankle Pogo — two pairs with a full reset and Plyo Push-Up — hands elevated: Use the prescribed small foot or hand contact to organize the next force action, then complete the full reset without extending the sequence.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Crossover recovery to a marked kick base** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Recover an exact useful two-foot base after reorientation, instead of leaving the feet crossed or too narrow. From a slightly open stance, take one short crossover recovery step, then uncross the feet as the pelvis turns into a premarked comfortable kick base. Stop for 2 s with both feet supporting. No knee lift, kick or jump; path stays within 2 m. Let the support foot or both feet turn freely with the pelvis; never force rotation through a trapped knee. Remain grounded. Use instead: less crossover distance and a wider base instead if foot placement is inconsistent; do not force a fast turn while crossed. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 11 · E6](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_11.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E2 | **Step-in rotational medicine-ball shot-put** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Retain a self-held entry-to-hand reference beside externally supplied tasks. Ball at rear shoulder; take one small lead-foot step, plant, pivot and push with the rear hand toward the low target. Hold the finish inside the bay. Use a light grippable ball and a verified low target in a permitted, fully contained release area; keep the entire flight, rebound and roll clear of people. Stay grounded, and do not receive a rebound. Keep all entry and finish footwork inside a cleared 1-metre start bay; no hop, jump or sprint. Use instead: the stationary shot-put instead if the step causes falling or knee twist. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 11 · E6](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_11.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E3 | **Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — fixed split stance, forward release** | **1 × 1 per leg** | Carry an asymmetric leg push into a free implement without another landing. Use a light ball between thighs and a small fixed split stance. Hinge slightly, drive through the feet to release forward/upward with long arms. No twist, step or catching return; perform one with each lead leg. Use instead: a parallel-stance hip scoop at the same total two throws if the split base twists; if containment fails, two unloaded rapid hip hinges with zero throws. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 2 total reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · E2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| E4 | **Medicine Ball Overhead Forward Throw — fixed stance, arm-driven** | **1 × 3** | Maintain a higher forward release path from an organized fixed base. Use a light ball and comfortable overhead range, ribs down; throw forward with both arms and remain planted. No lumbar whip or return catch. Use instead: a fixed-base chest pass if overhead range is not controlled or band speed pressing if containment is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · E5](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| E5 | **Ankle Pogo — two pairs with a full reset** | **1 × 4** | Reconnect a small ankle impulse once, then deliberately reset before repeating. Four pogos total, arranged as two consecutive low hops, stop and rest 15 s, then two more. Keep knees soft, travel negligible and absorb the second and fourth landings. No bonus bounce to restart. Use instead: four isolated supported rapid bilateral calf rises with 10 s resets if ankle rhythm or reception fails; record zero landing events. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** between-rep gaps 0 / 15 / 0 s; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · E5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| E6 | **Plyo Push-Up — hands elevated** | **1 × 3** | Integrate a brief push-off with a controlled two-hand reception at low volume. Inspected immovable support; controlled lowering, small simultaneous hand flight, soft reception and full reset. No clap or repeated rebound. Use instead: a non-flight fast incline push-up if actual hand-flight readiness or support fit is unconfirmed; count zero hand landings. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 12 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · E6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Light-Band Pallof Press — fixed split stance** | **1 × 4 per anchor side** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Control unwanted trunk rotation while force passes through a planted lead foot. Rated side attachment at chest height, light tension. Press hands forward 1 s, hold 2 s, return 2 s without rotating; change anchor-facing side after rest. Use instead: a light single-arm supine dumbbell press hold for 1 × 5 repetitions with a 2 s top hold per arm if no side attachment is available; record changed support. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between anchor side bouts; 8 total reps; 45 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · S1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| S2 | **Side-Lying Hip Adduction — light** | **1 × 4 per leg** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light medial-hip force to maintain a straight stance-leg push without drifting away from the foot. Top foot rests in front of the lower knee. Lift the straight lower leg slightly 2 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s; pelvis remains stacked. Use instead: a smaller controlled lower-leg lift at the same dose. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 8 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · S2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Split Squat — stationary, two dumbbells** | **1 × 4 per leg** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Independent lead-leg knee/hip extension with the rear foot supplying balance. Lower 3 s, rise in about 1 s without changing stance width; front whole foot stays loaded and pelvis stays square. Use instead: a supported split squat with lighter load or bodyweight at a challenging controlled depth. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between leg bouts; 8 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · P3](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| P2 | **Push-Up — controlled full repetition** | **1 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the press with free shoulder-blade movement and a steady trunk. Lower 2 s, push decisively, finish tall through shoulders for 1 s; no hand flight. Adjust leverage or band resistance to reach 2–3 RIR. Use instead: a hands-elevated push-up on an inspected immovable support. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · P3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| P3 | **Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees** | **1 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Plantar-flexion force while the knee remains flexed during early push and receiving. Sit on an inspected stable bench, feet on floor; secure loads on thighs, rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s without knee-angle change or bouncing. Use instead: a supported standing bent-knee calf raise with the same bilateral dose and 2–3 technical RIR if bench/load support is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · P5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Hammer Curl — simultaneous arms** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load elbow flexors for finishing the backward draw without another shoulder-extension set. Upper arms stay at ribs; lift 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s, without hip swing or wrist collapse. Use instead: a lighter neutral-grip curl without body movement. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 11 · P6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_11.md). |
| P5 | **Loaded Cossack Squat** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a substantial lateral knee-and-hip compound, strengthening weight acceptance and return over the stance leg that supports the selected lower rotational action. Hold a suitable dumbbell at the chest in a comfortable wide stance. Sit toward the working hip as the other leg lengthens, keeping the loaded foot supported and the knee following the toes. Return to centre under control. Use about 2 seconds lowering, 1 second lifting and 1 second organising the next rep. Depth is individually qualified; do not force a deep groin stretch or turn on a fixed knee. Use instead: a shallower controlled Cossack range and adjust the dumbbell load for knee or pelvic-position faults. Stop for groin pain or inability to qualify a challenging controlled range; do not add a second movement. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 11 · P3](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_11.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotational Press** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a compound unilateral push for the full-body force work while loading the connection from a freely turning foot and hip to a controlled arm press. Its diagonal pressing path complements the selected rotational task; it does not reproduce every throw path. Secure the landmine and qualify its bar, grip and minimum load. In a modest staggered stance, hold the sleeve near the rear shoulder with the rear hand. Turn the rear foot and hips into a controlled forward-and-up press, keeping wrist stacked and ribs level. No dip-drive, jump or release. Return in 3 seconds and reset without shoulder shrug or lower-back arch. Use instead: Reduce load or range for wrist, elbow or rib-position faults. If the minimum landmine setup is unsuitable, use a one-arm rotational band press from a verified anchor instead; log the changed load and setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 11 · P2](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_11.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

**Preparation sets, separate from working:**

- **P1 — Split Squat — stationary, two dumbbells:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per leg; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds, 30 s between legs; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P2 — Push-Up — controlled full repetition:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 3. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P3 — Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P4 — Dumbbell Hammer Curl — simultaneous arms:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P5 — Loaded Cossack Squat:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
- **P6 — Landmine Rotational Press:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

**Workload:** 5 nonrotational and 4 rotational ball releases, including 0 nonrotational downward slams and 0 rotational slams already included in those release totals; 0 source ball-receiving events and 0 rotational handoffs counted separately. 4 bilateral / 0 unilateral foot-landings (8 individual landing-foot contacts), with 4 low-amplitude events already included; 0 unilateral takeoffs. 3 bilateral hand-receptions / 6 hand contacts. 0 grounded rapid leg reps, 4 grounded lower-rotation efforts (including 0 kick actions) and 0 retained/unloaded upper-rotation efforts. 0 short starts / 0 accelerating metres; 0 counted lower source-force walking-entry steps and 0 counted upper source-force task steps. Rotational entry/recovery foot placements belong to complete rotational attempts and are not separately enumerated. Primary: 6 rounds / 9 side-specific bouts, 48 conventional/one-way-traverse reps plus 0 complete walkout cycles (0 heel placements). Preparation: 30 reps/traverses plus 0 walkout cycles, separate from work. Light supports: 16 reps/cycles. These mixed units are prescribed descriptions, not equivalent stress or actual completed work.

**Delivery and timing:** Estimate 57–64 minutes after Access & Prepare 1 with prompt equipment access. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Crossover recovery to a marked kick base emphasizes uncrossing into a marked rotational base; Step-in rotational medicine-ball shot-put retains a self-held step-to-press release instead of adding another supplied-ball sequence. The closing contact pair combines four low pogo receptions in two reset pairs with three separately reset hand receptions, so foot and hand receiving remain separate doses. Compared with Class 32, prescribed ball releases change from 0 to 9, bilateral foot-landing events change from 0 to 4, bilateral hand-receiving events change from 0 to 3. Check actual recovery and qualify foot placement and both receiving tasks independently; a marked base or accurate throw does not establish landing readiness. Reduce crossover distance for remaining E1 attempts when feet do not uncross cleanly. Use the listed isolated supported calf rises for remaining E5 attempts if rhythm fails, and nonflight incline push-ups for remaining E6 attempts if hand reception is unqualified; record each removed event type and preserve the specified resets.

Previous combined Class 32 prescribed 11 primary bouts and 55 conventional/traverse reps plus 2 cycles; this class prescribes 9 bouts, 48 reps and 0 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. This third lesson completes stage coverage and repeats the selected light supports for the stated shoulder/hip/foot jobs. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. The extra light supports are disclosed repeats rather than new work identities. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_33_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_33.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

**Handoff:** Review actual delivery and this saved workload before Class 34; develop its complementary job without assuming these prescriptions were performed.

- **E5 + E6 — Hip initiation into hand force:** Split-stance explosive hip pivot and Med Ball Rotational Scoop Toss — paused floor-target start: Repeat the combined Class 1 reduced-dose settled scoop and hip-pivot conditions when actual earlier conditions exist; no automatic improvement claim.

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |

| P1 | **Goblet Squat — dumbbell** | **1 × 5** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Bilateral knee/hip extension through a repeatable full-foot push. Lower 3 s, stand decisively in about 1 s; hold a manageable goblet load and retain comfortable depth without heels lifting. Use instead: a lighter goblet squat through controlled comfortable range; a two-dumbbell front hold may replace a limiting goblet grip. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P1](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |

Main horizontal pressing force with a reproducible floor-limited range. Lower 2 s, touch upper arms gently, pause 1 s, then press decisively; feet stay down. Coach supervises pickup/set-down. Use instead: a lighter floor press through controlled comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 150 s between complete set rounds; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P1](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |

Plantar-flexion force with the knees extended for the final upward/forward push. Use stable hand support if needed; rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s to the floor. Keep knees softly straight and heels tracking over the forefoot. Use instead: a lighter supported standing calf raise; use individual load or leverage that retains the required technical reserve. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P4](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Rear-Delt Row — bilateral, hinged** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Strengthen the wider-elbow backward draw that controls the arm-open position. Hold a still hinge; elbows about 60 degrees from torso, pull to upper ribs, hold 1 s, lower 2 s. Use instead: a chest-supported version only on an inspected bench with adequate arm clearance. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P4](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |

Load controlled internal-rotation torque directly at the hip, complementing the larger leg push used to organise the selected grounded lower turn. Sit on a stable bench with thighs supported and hips and knees near 90 degrees. Fit a secure loop around the lower shins. Keep the other foot planted while the working foot moves a short distance outward, lightly clear of the floor, through hip internal rotation. Keep that knee and the pelvis still. Take 2 seconds outward and 2 seconds returning; do not force end range or twist the knee or ankle. Use instead: Reduce range or band tension for knee, ankle or pelvic movement. If the loop cannot be fitted securely, use self-resisted seated hip internal rotation with the opposite hand resisting the outside of the lower shin instead; manual resistance is a changed and less reproducible load. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 12 · P4](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotation** | **1 × 6 alternating traverses total** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the turning arc and controlled endpoints that transmit lower-body force into the selected upper rotational action. Secure the landmine and qualify the bar, grip and minimum load. Hold the sleeve or suitable grip in both hands with elbows softly bent. Turn the feet and hips with a modest side-to-side loaded arc while keeping ribs over pelvis. Each one-way traverse is one rep; the return is the next counted rep. Take about 2 seconds per traverse and settle briefly at each endpoint. Avoid trunk lean, a trapped knee or an uncontrolled finish. Use instead: Reduce the load or arc for loss of alignment. If a secured, manageable landmine setup cannot be qualified, use a standing two-hand band rotation with a verified side anchor and the same one-way traverse counting instead; log the changed setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** Finish this working bout; no additional set or finisher is prescribed. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 12 · P1](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

- **P1 — Goblet Squat — dumbbell:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 5 then 1 × 3; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 90 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P2 — Floor Press — two dumbbells, neutral grip:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 6 then 1 × 3. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 90 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P3 — Standing Dumbbell Calf Raise — straight knees:** Load preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4; very light first, intermediate only if a second set is listed; at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; 60 s between preparation rounds; 60 s before working sets. Extra load-finding work adds actual workload/time.
- **P4 — Dumbbell Rear-Delt Row — bilateral, hinged:** Load preparation, separate from working sets: 1 × 5. Start very light; a second set is intermediate. Keep at least 5 technical reps in reserve. Rest 60 seconds between preparation rounds, then 60 seconds before working sets. Extra load-finding reps add actual workload and time.
- **P5 — Seated band hip internal rotation:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 3 per side, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 30 s between sides; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.
- **P6 — Landmine Rotation:** Preparation of this same lift: 1 × 4 alternating one-way traverses total, very light at ≥5 technical RIR; 60 s before working. Use the same controlled movement/return tempo; extra ramps add actual work and time.

**Workload:** 9 nonrotational and 4 rotational ball releases, including 0 nonrotational downward slams and 0 rotational slams already included in those release totals; 0 source ball-receiving events and 0 rotational handoffs counted separately. 6 bilateral / 0 unilateral foot-landings (12 individual landing-foot contacts), with 0 low-amplitude events already included; 0 unilateral takeoffs. 0 bilateral hand-receptions / 0 hand contacts. 3 grounded rapid leg reps, 4 grounded lower-rotation efforts (including 0 kick actions) and 0 retained/unloaded upper-rotation efforts. 0 short starts / 0 accelerating metres; 0 counted lower source-force walking-entry steps and 0 counted upper source-force task steps. Rotational entry/recovery foot placements belong to complete rotational attempts and are not separately enumerated. Primary: 7 rounds / 8 side-specific bouts, 47 conventional/one-way-traverse reps plus 0 complete walkout cycles (0 heel placements). Preparation: 36 reps/traverses plus 0 walkout cycles, separate from work. Light supports: 10 reps/cycles. These mixed units are prescribed descriptions, not equivalent stress or actual completed work.

**Delivery and timing:** Estimate 63–70 minutes after Access & Prepare 1 with prompt equipment access. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Existing preparation and complete booking duration remain unresolved. Never shorten needed recovery to fit a clock. Class numbers specify 36 ordered exposures, not consecutive days or a fixed 36-week calendar. Review actual sport, lifting, throwing, kicking, running, jumping and separate tumbling before scheduling. Roughly 48–72 hours between comparably demanding exposures is an initial planning allowance, adjusted to actual recovery. Do not also perform the standalone source workouts or double up missed classes. No athlete completion or readiness is inferred from a saved class.

**Setup:** Inspect all loads, stable supports, band/landmine attachments, surfaces and athlete fit. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets. Before multiple substitutions, check that they do not duplicate another slot; otherwise stop the affected work and record a modified/incomplete delivery for revision.

**Progression note:** Squat Jump — settled quarter-squat start and Medicine Ball Chest Pass — stationary, paused restore the combined opening class's first two tasks, order and anchor doses: six settled-start jumps and nine stationary chest releases. The small hip pivot and paused rotational scoop are familiar support references; their preceding accessory work differs from Class 1, so they do not by themselves form a controlled before/after test. Compared with Class 33, prescribed ball releases change from 9 to 13, bilateral foot-landing events change from 4 to 6, bilateral hand-receiving events change from 3 to 0. Delivery requires actual recovery and matched recorded setup, surface, ball, technique, preparation and rest before comparing the opening anchors. If those earlier observations do not exist, record current quality only. Use the listed smaller jump or nonflight rise for remaining E1 attempts and the secured bilateral band chest press for remaining E2 attempts when release containment fails; mark changed comparison conditions and add no test attempts.

Previous combined Class 33 prescribed 9 primary bouts and 48 conventional/traverse reps plus 0 cycles; this class prescribes 8 bouts, 47 reps and 0 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

**Audit summary:** All 14 slots have a specific job, nonzero dose, cue, recovery and replacement. The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. Each retained source-force entry keeps its original set/rep/side dose, and added rotation uses a separate reduced dose with original provenance. No rotational landing is added. Similar muscle groups receive complementary initiation, transfer and finishing jobs; the light supports stay easy. Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_34_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_34.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

## Full Body Force Generation — Class 35 of 36

**Stage 12, lesson B: Review fixed-base independent force**

**Primary effort:** Review independent arm force and a grounded hip-driven projection with controlled starts.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Single-Arm Band Chest Press — still start on a spoken cue as the controlled upper-body force anchor. Review independent arm force and a grounded hip-driven projection with controlled starts. Review current quality under matched recorded conditions where available; do not infer improvement from absent baseline data.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Single-Arm Band Chest Press — still start on a spoken cue. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. For a repeated source reference, compare only an actually recorded earlier combined occurrence with matching dose, actual equipment, stance, arm strategy, range, reset, side/set rest, preparation and order. An unmatched or missing baseline is not zero or improvement. E1 matches the prescribed execution/dose in combined Class 32; E2 matches the prescribed execution/dose in combined Class 11; E5 matches the prescribed execution/dose in combined Class 2. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Clear start with retained support:** Single-Arm Band Chest Press — still start on a spoken cue and Kettlebell Swing — dead-stop two-hand repetitions: Set the support and load before the cued independent press or parked hip impulse, retaining the source task's declared trunk behavior.
- **E3 + E4 — Independent draw and leg-driven release:** One-Arm Row — supported mid-range pause into fast finish and Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — hip-driven forward release: Keep the support and torso organized while the arm completes its held-start draw or the hips supply the forward scoop, without adding an unrecorded turn.
- **E5 + E6 — Supported rotational force:** Standing low roundhouse shadow kick and Med Ball Shot-Put Throw — rotational floor-target version: A short rotational pushing release and familiar rear-leg low kick revisit direct endpoints at reduced source dose.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Single-Arm Band Chest Press — still start on a spoken cue** | **1 × 2 per arm** | Retain organized independent force onset without additional ball or hand-impact exposure. Self-anchored band, other hand secures tail at torso; wait 2–4 s for a simple cue, press rapidly and return 2 s without trunk rotation. Use instead: a self-initiated single-arm band speed press if cue timing reduces control. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between arm bouts; 4 total reps; 5 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · E2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| E2 | **Kettlebell Swing — dead-stop two-hand repetitions** | **1 × 3** | Rapid hip extension moves a retained implement without adding landings. Hike a light kettlebell from a stable parked start, snap hips to a tall finish with a chest-level float, guide the return and park fully after each rep. No squat pull, arm lift or lumbar arch. Use instead: a very light rapid dumbbell hip hinge with a 2 s lowering and still finish if swing skill or grip is unconfirmed. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 10 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · E2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| E3 | **One-Arm Row — supported mid-range pause into fast finish** | **1 × 2 per arm** | Express each arm's final draw without borrowing force from the trunk. Light dumbbell, hand/knee supported; hold with elbow partly bent for 2 s, draw toward hip quickly, then lower fully for 2 s before resetting. No shoulder roll. Use instead: a full-range light supported speed row if the partial start feels strained. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between arm bouts; 4 total reps; 5 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · E3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| E4 | **Medicine Ball Scoop Toss — hip-driven forward release** | **1 × 2** | Transfer leg/hip extension into a freely released implement rather than a retained swing. Light ball between thighs, soft knees and hinge; drive hips tall and project forward/upward with long arms. Feet stay down, no trunk turn or catching return. Let the ball stop before retrieval. Use instead: two rapid light-dumbbell quarter-squat rises with heels grounded, 10 s between reps and the same next-section recovery; keep arms fixed and drive the load with the legs; log zero throws. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · E5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| E5 | **Standing low roundhouse shadow kick** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Revisit the low kick execution and dose used in combined Class 2. Preceding exercises differ, so use current movement quality unless actual equipment, preparation, order and recovery are also documented as matching. Use a short fighting stance with the kicking leg rear and a low kick line. Turn the support foot with the pelvis, accelerate the free thigh, extend only through a controlled partial arc, actively recoil and return to the same stance with a 2-second hold. Keep the foot below hip height and the terminal knee soft. A qualified coach must establish the low noncontact execution. Keep the foot below hip height, support foot turning with the pelvis, knee softly extended and recoil active; no target impact, jump, spin or locked-knee snap. Use instead: a shorter low arc and less knee extension if control or recoil is insufficient; record changed execution instead of claiming a matched comparison. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 12 · E2](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E6 | **Med Ball Shot-Put Throw — rotational floor-target version** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Revisit the unilateral pushing release under controlled standing conditions. Ball supported beside the rear shoulder, opposite foot forward. Pivot the rear foot/hip and drive the rear hand through the target; guide hand releases and no arm winds behind the shoulder. Use a light grippable ball and a verified low target in a permitted, fully contained release area; keep the entire flight, rebound and roll clear of people. Stay grounded, and do not receive a rebound. Use instead: a lighter grippable ball if wrist alignment or release speed fails. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 12 · E3](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

Stop range before back rounding or pelvic tuck. Use instead: a lighter two-dumbbell hinge through a shorter controlled range. Use instead: a thigh-supported dumbbell row with the same arm path if a bench does not fit. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s between complete set rounds; 60 s between arm bouts; 24 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |

Knee-flexor leverage while the pelvis stays raised, distinct from moving through a hip hinge. Start in a bilateral bridge. Each cycle is two small heel steps out per foot, then two back per foot: eight placements in about 8 s. Keep a soft knee and level hips; never chase a fully straight leg. Use instead: a shorter-lever walkout at the same complete-cycle count; reduce range for sagging or cramp and stop if control does not return. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P5](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |

If the loop cannot be secured, use self-resisted seated bent-knee hip flexion with the hands resisting the distal thigh instead; record the changed, less reproducible resistance. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 12 · P5](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotational Press** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a compound unilateral push for the full-body force work while loading the connection from a freely turning foot and hip to a controlled arm press. Its diagonal pressing path complements the selected rotational task; it does not reproduce every throw path. Secure the landmine and qualify its bar, grip and minimum load. In a modest staggered stance, hold the sleeve near the rear shoulder with the rear hand. Turn the rear foot and hips into a controlled forward-and-up press, keeping wrist stacked and ribs level. No dip-drive, jump or release. Return in 3 seconds and reset without shoulder shrug or lower-back arch. Use instead: Reduce load or range for wrist, elbow or rib-position faults. If the minimum landmine setup is unsuitable, use a one-arm rotational band press from a verified anchor instead; log the changed load and setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 12 · P2](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Includes selected work, resets, side/set recovery, new-order transitions, same-lift preparation and 12–18 minutes extra instruction/setup. Additional recovery, queues or load finding add actual time. Use the three separate 10 m lanes with complete setup, action and receiving/stopping envelopes inside the available space; do not combine them into a 30 m runway. Clear waiting/return paths outside active ball and swept-leg areas. Ball flight, impact, bounce and roll containment require a separately verified area; no throwing wall or rebound behavior is assumed. Only tasks that explicitly prescribe a feed or receiving action include it; all other releases are no-catch. Retrieve only after the ball stops and the coach clears entry into the area. Low kicks are noncontact and below hip height, with active recoil and no knee snap or spin; qualify kicking competence during the prescribed first attempts. For a handoff, secure the ball and let the feeder release and step clear before turning; no airborne feed is added. Finish each exercise and its recovery, then clear/reset the area before the next. Mechanical connections do not authorize simultaneous jump/kick/throw combinations or supersets.
