The parked hip impulse and small forward scoop retain familiar force routes; the returning low roundhouse uses a familiar task and dose, but preceding work differs from its earlier prescription. Compared with Class 34, prescribed ball releases change from 13 to 6, bilateral foot-landing events change from 6 to 0, kick actions change from 0 to 4. Review actual recovery, require the held row without shoulder roll and the kick to recoil into a balanced stance, and compare only fully documented like conditions. Use a self-initiated press for remaining E1 attempts if the cue disrupts control, a full-range supported speed row for remaining E3 attempts if the partial start is strained, and a shorter kick arc for remaining E5 attempts when needed. Log those as changed executions, not matched improvement.

Previous combined Class 34 prescribed 8 primary bouts and 47 conventional/traverse reps plus 0 cycles; this class prescribes 11 bouts, 57 reps and 2 cycles. This is a redistributed task dose, not an earned load increase. Original force doses/within-task recovery and same-lift preparation are retained; rotational additions are separately reduced. Advance only after actual controlled execution at the target reserve and satisfactory recovery. Hold, reduce or revise if feedback or first-attempt quality does not support the new constraint.

The six explosive slots include all four requested sources; the first task and three connections define the day's effort. ## Full Body Force Generation — Class 36 of 36

**Stage 12, lesson C: Review lead-leg and arm sequence**

**Primary effort:** Review a low hip-led rotational kick with lateral recovery and a retained upper-body rotational pull. Supported lead-leg work and alternating or independent forward arm actions provide small linear-force support; no improvement is inferred without matched actual records.

**Baseline:** Ages 12–14 with established lifting/landing technique, individually qualified ballistic/kicking skill and supervision. All athlete execution, load and recovery remain unreported.

**Daily stimulus brief:** Open with Low roundhouse with lateral recovery placement as the grounded rotational force anchor. Control the return leg when restoring a base slightly off the original line after rotational output. Review current quality under matched recorded conditions where available; do not infer improvement from absent baseline data.

**Quality marker:** Main observation: Low roundhouse with lateral recovery placement. Within its listed attempts, record the declared starting position, side, movement/receiving control and a stable finish. Also record the partner task in the first connection under its own criteria; do not treat unlike drills as equivalent force tests. For a repeated source reference, compare only an actually recorded earlier combined occurrence with matching dose, actual equipment, stance, arm strategy, range, reset, side/set rest, preparation and order. An unmatched or missing baseline is not zero or improvement. E2 matches the prescribed execution/dose in combined Class 8; E3 matches the prescribed execution/dose in combined Class 18; E6 matches the prescribed execution/dose in combined Class 3. A miss consumes the attempt; no additional testing slots or retries are added.

**Connections:**

- **E1 + E2 — Turn and control the finish:** Low roundhouse with lateral recovery placement and Rotational band row speed drive: Retained upper pulling turn plus only one kick/side with lateral placement finishes the course without increasing the source kick dose.
- **E3 + E4 — Supported rise and alternating finish:** Step-Up — fast grounded drive, rear foot joins and Floor Press — alternating light speed presses, opposite arm parked: Maintain a stable body base while the lead leg rises or the arms alternate their presses, keeping the opposite support or load controlled.
- **E5 + E6 — Independent force from organized support:** Single-Leg Squat Rise — supported rapid quarter-range and Medicine Ball Shot Put Throw — forward-facing, no rotation: Keep the supporting leg and trunk organized as the single-leg rise or forward-facing arm release finishes, without adding rotation to either source task.

### 1. Access & Prepare 1

Complete the existing predetermined Access & Prepare 1, unchanged and supplied separately. Its exercises, duration and workload are not recreated or assumed. Review its actual demands before this full-body class.

### 2. Explosiveness — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six separate explosive tasks: two upper force, two lower force, one upper rotation and one lower rotation. The opener is today’s main quality observation; every other task has a specific supporting job. Use bodyweight/light resistance and rapid intent with controlled execution, preserving the written reset and side/set recovery. Extend rest to 2–3 minutes when needed. A miss consumes the attempt; no bonus test or make-up reps.

Stop for pain, unsafe implement path, trapped support knee, uncontrolled reception, lost alignment or clear slowing. Regress only remaining work when control returns; otherwise log the stopped slot. Source alternative doses are capped by the remaining combined dose. No new flight is added by the rotational selections.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| E1 | **Low roundhouse with lateral recovery placement** | **1 × 1 per side/direction** | Control the return leg when restoring a base slightly off the original line after rotational output. From a stationary rear-leg stance, deliver one low roundhouse, fold the knee and place the returning foot into a short lateral recovery stance instead of its original rear footprint. Finish with the pelvis/feet aligned and no follow-on shuffle or kick. A qualified coach must establish the low noncontact execution. Keep the foot below hip height, support foot turning with the pelvis, knee softly extended and recoil active; no target impact, jump, spin or locked-knee snap. Use instead: a smaller kick arc and recovery width instead if the foot crosses behind the support knee or the athlete keeps spinning. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between side/direction bouts; 2 total reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 12 · E6](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E2 | **Rotational band row speed drive** | **1 × 2 per side/direction** | Keep the pulling-side acceleration represented with little final added volume. Light band ahead at lower-chest height. From a small reach, pivot and draw the elbow toward the ribs quickly, then return in 3 seconds with a quiet shoulder. Verify the stated band anchor and tension; retain the band throughout, with no release or uncontrolled recoil. Use instead: Reduce tension/range if the shoulder hikes or trunk yanks. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side/direction bouts; 4 total reps; 15 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 12 · E6](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** Rotate source drills into one small support slot beside four sagittal force entries; Stage 6 consolidation is reduced again; never increase a source dose already below the combined cap. |
| E3 | **Step-Up — fast grounded drive, rear foot joins** | **1 × 1 per leg** | Let one lead leg supply upward force against a stable support without hand-off to a jump. Use a low inspected immovable step. Front whole foot planted; rise quickly without springing from the trailing foot, bring that foot onto the step, then descend under control in 3 s. No flight. Use instead: a supported fast split-squat rise with both feet grounded and the same per-leg dose if support fit fails. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 2 total reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · E4](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| E4 | **Floor Press — alternating light speed presses, opposite arm parked** | **1 × 4** | Coordinate alternating forward arm force while retaining control of the other load. Both dumbbells start with upper arms gently on floor. Press one arm quickly while the other remains parked at the floor boundary, lower 2 s, then switch; four total presses, two per arm. No simultaneous lift, torso roll or weight release. Use instead: two simultaneous bilateral light floor-press cycles if alternating load handling is unsuitable; record changed cycle counting. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 5 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · E4](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| E5 | **Single-Leg Squat Rise — supported rapid quarter-range** | **1 × 1 per leg** | Express stance-leg knee extension without flight or a rear foot contributing. Hold stable support, free foot clear; lower to a shallow controlled squat for 2 s, hold 1 s and rise quickly through the whole foot. Use the hand for balance/needed assistance, not a sudden pull. Use instead: a supported split-squat rapid rise at the same per-leg dose when the single-leg position cannot stay organized. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 45 s between leg bouts; 2 total reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · E6](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| E6 | **Medicine Ball Shot Put Throw — forward-facing, no rotation** | **1 × 2 per arm** | Review current independent-arm projection with a simple fixed-base task. Manageable ball at shoulder; fixed split stance, opposite foot forward, no deliberate rotation or step. Release forward and let the ball stop. Use instead: a secured single-arm band speed press when ball fit or containment is unsuitable; log the release gap. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between arm bouts; 4 total reps; 20 s between individual reps; 90 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · E6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |

### 3. Strength (Stabilization/Resilience) — Exactly 2 Exercises

Exactly two light strengthening tasks, one for each region. Keep at least 5 technical reps/cycles in reserve; do not chase burn or fatigue. Preserve the source tempo and any counted hold/relaxation. Stop or reduce range/load if control fades.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | **Bilateral Band External Rotation — elbows at sides** | **1 × 8** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light shoulder-rotator strengthening to control upper-arm position during the day's press and pull. Unanchored band between hands, elbows at ribs; rotate outward 2 s, hold 1 s, return 2 s without shrugging. Use instead: a lower-tension band and shorter comfortable range. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · S2](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| S2 | **Side-Lying Hip Abduction — light** | **1 × 4 per leg** | Keep this light support at ≥5 technical RIR. Light lateral-hip strengthening to keep the pelvis and knee organized over the force-producing leg. Lie on side, lower leg bent; lift upper leg slightly 2 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s with toes facing forward and pelvis still. Bodyweight is sufficient unless clearly easy. Use instead: a smaller unloaded leg lift with the same per-leg dose. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 30 s between leg bouts; 8 total reps; 60 s before the next exercise/section. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · S2](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |

### 4. Strength (Primary) — Exactly 6 Exercises

Six challenging primary-strength tasks: two from each force course plus one upper and one lower rotational support. Use about 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve with controlled lowering/range. No forced reps, failure/max testing or breathless circuit. Same-lift preparation is separate from working sets; extra load finding adds actual work/time.

The original force-course doses and preparation stay with their source exercises. Added rotational support uses the clearly stated smaller combined dose and its own easy preparation. A new class or changed position in the sequence does not justify a load increase. Judge late-session technical reserve and record reductions rather than forcing nominal completion.

| # | Exercise | Sets × reps | Specific job in this workout |
|---|---|---|---|
| P1 | **Split Squat — stationary, two dumbbells** | **1 × 4 per leg** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Independent lead-leg knee/hip extension with the rear foot supplying balance. Lower 3 s, rise in about 1 s without changing stance width; front whole foot stays loaded and pelvis stays square. Use instead: a supported split squat with lighter load or bodyweight at a challenging controlled depth. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 60 s between leg bouts; 8 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P3](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| P2 | **Push-Up — controlled full repetition** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the press with free shoulder-blade movement and a steady trunk. Lower 2 s, push decisively, finish tall through shoulders for 1 s; no hand flight. Adjust leverage or band resistance to reach 2–3 RIR. Use instead: a hands-elevated push-up on an inspected immovable support. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P3](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| P3 | **Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise — bent knees** | **1 × 6** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Plantar-flexion force while the knee remains flexed during early push and receiving. Sit on an inspected stable bench, feet on floor; secure loads on thighs, rise 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s without knee-angle change or bouncing. Use instead: a supported standing bent-knee calf raise with the same bilateral dose and 2–3 technical RIR if bench/load support is unsuitable. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P5](../lower_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| P4 | **Dumbbell Hammer Curl — simultaneous arms** | **1 × 8** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load elbow flexors for finishing the backward draw without another shoulder-extension set. Upper arms stay at ribs; lift 1 s, hold 1 s, lower 2 s, without hip swing or wrist collapse. Use instead: a lighter neutral-grip curl without body movement. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Upper Body Force Generation · source Class 12 · P6](../upper_body_force_generation/classes/class_12.md). |
| P5 | **Loaded Cossack Squat** | **1 × 5 per side/direction** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Supply a substantial lateral knee-and-hip compound, strengthening weight acceptance and return over the stance leg that supports the selected lower rotational action. Hold a suitable dumbbell at the chest in a comfortable wide stance. Sit toward the working hip as the other leg lengthens, keeping the loaded foot supported and the knee following the toes. Return to centre under control. Use about 2 seconds lowering, 1 second lifting and 1 second organising the next rep. Depth is individually qualified; do not force a deep groin stretch or turn on a fixed knee. Use instead: a shallower controlled Cossack range and adjust the dumbbell load for knee or pelvic-position faults. Stop for groin pain or inability to qualify a challenging controlled range; do not add a second movement. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** 90 s between side bouts; 10 total reps; 120 s before the next lift preparation. **Source:** [Lower-body rotational power · source Class 12 · P3](../rotational_explosiveness_lower_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |
| P6 | **Landmine Rotation** | **1 × 6 alternating traverses total** | Keep the working load at 2–3 technical reps/cycles in reserve. Load the turning arc and controlled endpoints that transmit lower-body force into the selected upper rotational action. Secure the landmine and qualify the bar, grip and minimum load. Hold the sleeve or suitable grip in both hands with elbows softly bent. Turn the feet and hips with a modest side-to-side loaded arc while keeping ribs over pelvis. Each one-way traverse is one rep; the return is the next counted rep. Take about 2 seconds per traverse and settle briefly at each endpoint. Avoid trunk lean, a trapped knee or an uncontrolled finish. Use instead: Reduce the load or arc for loss of alignment. If a secured, manageable landmine setup cannot be qualified, use a standing two-hand band rotation with a verified side anchor and the same one-way traverse counting instead; log the changed setup. Apply this change only to the same remaining combined dose and recovery; it replaces the original execution and adds no extra task or make-up attempts. This replacement uses only the remaining combined dose/rest unless explicitly stated; log changed support, event type, load and added time. **Recovery:** Finish this working bout; no additional set or finisher is prescribed. **Source:** [Upper-body rotational power · source Class 12 · P1](../rotational_explosiveness_upper_body/classes/class_12.md). **Combined dose:** One smaller challenging support set beside four retained force-primary entries; no automatic load increase. Stage 6 uses four reps per side or four alternating traverses. |

Working/preparation time was recalculated after reordering; old source phase transitions are replaced by today's explicit recovery. Full duration and athlete response remain unknown. See [class audit](audits/class_36_audit.md), [workload](workload_class_36.json) and [stage coverage](source_coverage.json).

**Handoff:** All 36 classes are authored. Review actual matched output, loads, technical reserve, substitutions and recovery before selecting the next cycle; do not create Class 37 automatically.
